Motivation: LLM隐藏状态虽然是普通向量，但其向量间的距离可能仍然显示层级结构。作者希望系统性地考察当代LLM中隐藏状态的距离结构是否具有树状特性，以理解语言模型内部的表示组织方式，并探索GH作为可解释性诊断工具的潜力。

Method: 使用818,904个样本-层测量，对十个开源权重模型在四个基准数据集（MATH500、HumanEval、WinoGrande、TruthfulQA）上的提示词令牌隐藏状态进行Gromov双曲性分析。构建GH图谱，系统比较四个维度：参数规模、层深度、模型家族和输入域。

Result: 最清晰的发现是层深度的影响大于参数规模：中间层形成高相对双曲性稳定平台，而网络最后层往往变得更树状（双曲性更高）。规模效应微弱且非单调；同为7B/8B的不同模型家族在GH上差异显著；不同输入域与模型的领域特化相互影响。

Conclusion: GH可以作为实用的诊断工具，揭示层级距离结构在何处出现、领域特化如何改变它，以及哪些模型-层-域的组合值得更深入分析。这为LLM的层级结构与知识组织研究提供了新的度量视角。

Abstract: LLM hidden states are ordinary vectors, but the distances among those vectors may still show hierarchical structure. To our knowledge, this paper is the first systematic study of whether prompt-token hidden states in contemporary LLMs exhibit Gromov Hyperbolicity (GH), a distance-based measure of tree-likeness. Using 818,904 sample-layer measurements from ten open-weight models across MATH500, HumanEval, WinoGrande, and TruthfulQA, we build a GH map over four axes: parameter scale, layer depth, model family, and input domain. The clearest pattern is depth, not scale: middle layers usually form a high-relative-hyperbolicity plateau, while final layers often become substantially more tree-like. Scale effects are weak and non-monotonic, matched 7/8B model families differ strongly, and domains interact with model specialization. These findings make GH useful as a practical diagnostic: it shows where hierarchical distance structure appears, how specialization changes it, and which model-layer-domain comparisons deserve closer analysis.

</details>


### [166] [Where to Look and What to Use: Retrieve-Localize-Generate for Long-Term Conversational Memory Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07093)
*Yifan Wang,Xinkui Lin,Yongxiu Xu,Shen Gao,Ruochen Yang,Kun Huang,Yubin Wang,Jie Wu,Wei Liu,Jian Luan,Hongbo Xu,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MemLoc框架，通过统一的"检索-定位-生成"流程解决长期对话记忆问答中证据碎片化和丢失中间效应两大挑战，在四个基准上取得最优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RAG的长期会话记忆问答方法面临两大关键挑战：(1) 相关证据碎片化地散布在时间上相距较远的会话中，难以被有效检索；(2) 检索到的会话内部包含噪声内容，会触发LLM的"丢失中间"效应，降低回答质量。

Method: MemLoc包含三个核心组件：检索阶段将每个会话分解为多粒度记忆单元，通过内部记忆图进行查询路由与基于熵的粒度选择，并通过跨记忆图建模跨会话的语义和时间依赖，实现从粗到精的Top-K记忆候选检索；定位阶段提出基于推理的证据定位器，使用自反思提示策略优化（SHPO）训练，通过渐进式精炼提取与查询相关的片段以抑制噪声，并跨候选重排序消除冗余，最终生成带有轻量定位ID的紧凑证据集；生成阶段利用定位ID作为精确的接地信号引导LLM定位正确记忆位置，既缓解丢失中间效应又保留原始上下文完整性。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MemLoc在检索准确率和回答质量方面均达到最先进水平，同时保持了较高的运行效率。

Conclusion: MemLoc通过将检索、定位和生成统一到一个框架中，有效解决了长期会话记忆问答中证据碎片化和噪声导致丢失中间效应的问题，验证了推理式证据定位与轻量定位ID引导策略的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) enables large language models (LLMs) to answer questions by accessing external knowledge and has been widely adopted for long-term conversational memory question answering. However, existing methods suffer from two key challenges: (1) fragmented evidence scattered across temporally distant sessions, and (2) noisy content within retrieved sessions that triggers the lost-in-the-middle effect. To address these challenges, we propose MemLoc, a unified Retrieve-Localize-Generate framework for long-term conversational memory QA. For retrieval, MemLoc decomposes each session into multi-granularity memory units and performs query routing via an inner-memory graph with entropy-based granularity selection. It further models cross-session semantic and temporal dependencies through a cross-memory graph, enabling coarse-to-fine retrieval of top-K relevant memory candidates. For localization, we introduce a reasoning-based evidence locator trained with Self-reflective Hint Policy Optimization (SHPO), which performs progressive refinement by extracting query-relevant fragments within memory units to suppress noise and reranking across candidates to remove redundancy, producing a compact evidence set with lightweight location IDs. For generation, these IDs act as precise grounding signals that guide the LLM to the correct memory positions, mitigating the lost-in-the-middle effect while preserving original contextual integrity. Extensive experiments on four benchmarks demonstrate that MemLoc achieves state-of-the-art retrieval accuracy and response quality while maintaining efficiency. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Nikol-coder/MemLoc.

</details>


### [167] [Revisiting Complete Reasoning Traces for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07103)
*Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Byeongho Heo,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在监督微调中学习完整推理轨迹的收益有限，而仅用推理端点（输入-答案对）训练即可获得更强效果，并证明中间推理token具有高度冗余性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练常依赖预收集的完整推理轨迹（往往冗长且包含绕路），但尚未深入探究LLM是否真的受益于学习完整的推理轨迹，作者希望重新审视该前提。

Method: 通过三方面验证：1）对比完整轨迹与部分（截断）轨迹的SFT效果；2）使用基于注意力的分析与受控token移除实验来评估中间token的贡献；3）在RL与on-policy蒸馏等后训练方法上进一步验证基于端点的训练效果。

Result: 完整轨迹收益有限，而部分轨迹即使在重度截断下仍然有效；注意力与token移除实验表明中间token对最终推理质量贡献极小；基于端点训练引起推理行为的一致性改变，并对RL及on-policy蒸馏同样有效。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹中存在高度冗余信息，去除冗余有助于让LLM依靠内部知识补全缺失步骤，提示领域需要重新审视并重设计基于完整推理轨迹的后训练范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often post-trained on pre-collected reasoning trajectories to improve their reasoning capability. Such trajectories tend to be long due to complex, interwoven paths, which often include detours on the path toward the answer. However, it has been underexplored whether LLMs indeed benefit from learning complete trajectories in post-training, such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Starting from our pilot study, we find that full trajectories provide only limited benefit, while partial trajectories are effective even under heavy truncation. We analyze redundancy in reasoning trajectories through attention-based analyses and controlled token-removal studies, both of which show that intermediate tokens contribute minimally to final reasoning quality. This suggests that avoiding redundant information may allow LLMs to internally infer coherent alternatives by inferring missing steps from their internal knowledge, given known trajectory endpoints. Furthermore, we show that training LLMs using endpoints leads to consistent changes in reasoning behavior, and that it also benefits post-training methods based on reinforcement learning or on-policy distillation, highlighting the need to revisit complete reasoning traces. Code is available at https://github.com/naver-ai/revisiting-trace.

</details>


### [168] [The Illusion of Debiasing: Persona Steering Redistributes Rather Than Reduces Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07117)
*Ziyue Feng,Hongbo Fang,James A. Evans*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用人格条件作用作为受控探针，沿深度轴（自我报告→开放式生成→词汇级参数联想）测量系统提示对三种指令微调模型的影响，发现层级分离现象：人格提示在表面可读但非结构性的，模型能遵循单特征指令却无法复现人类特征间协方差，且随着测量深度增加分离加深——人格提示保持或放大闭式QA偏差、改变绝对语气但不改变组间差异、几乎不扰动已饱和的联想基线。提示引导主要作用于输出通道，存在表面可操作可掩盖的结构触及上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者质疑基于提示的干预（系统提示、人设、角色指令）虽然能可靠地重塑语言模型的输出，但其作用机制尚不明确——它们是否真正重构了模型的内部结构，还是仅仅调节了输出通道？此前缺乏沿深度维度系统评估提示影响的研究。

Method: 以人格条件作用作为受控探针，在三个指令微调模型上沿深度轴测量效果，该轴从自我报告、开放式生成到词汇级参数联想逐层深入。通过比较单特征指令遵循与人类特质间协方差复现，以及分析闭式QA偏差、绝对语气与组间差异、词汇联想基线等指标来考察提示的触及深度。

Result: 发现梯度式的分离：（1）人格在表面"可读但非结构性"——模型遵循单特征指令但无法复现人类特质间的协方差模式；（2）分离随深度加深——闭式QA中人格保持或放大偏差，开放式生成中只改变绝对语气而组间差异不变，词汇联想层面几乎不扰动已饱和的基线。表明提示引导主要作用于输出通道。

Conclusion: 基于提示的引导（prompt-based steering）存在结构触及上限，其操作局限于输出通道，而这种表面可操作性（surface manipulability）会掩盖其内部结构影响力的不足。这一发现对理解提示工程的内在限制及语言模型可操控性边界具有重要意义。

Abstract: Prompt-based interventions: system prompts, personas, role instructions, reliably reshape what a language model says, but it is unclear which layer they reach. Do they reconfigure internal structure, or only modulate the output channel? We use persona conditioning as a controlled probe, measuring its effects along a depth axis from self-report, through open-ended generation, to word-level parametric association, across three instruction-tuned models. We find a graded dissociation. Personas are legible but not structural: models follow single-trait instructions yet fail to reproduce human inter-trait covariance. The dissociation deepens with depth: personas hold or amplify closed-form QA bias, shift absolute tone while leaving between-group disparity unchanged, and barely perturb an already saturated associative baseline. Prompt-based steering thus operates in the output channel and has a structural reach limit that surface manipulability can mask.

</details>


### [169] [PTCG: Persona-guided Tree-based Counterargument Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07120)
*Eunbeen Son,Yohan Jo,Joonsuk Park,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PTCG框架，结合Tree-of-Thoughts启发式逐步生成/剪枝与说话人个性选择，通过估计原作者个性并融入代表不同视角的说话人个性来实现换位思考，从而生成多样且具说服力的反驳论点，在多类评估中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反驳论点生成方法通常只生成单一反方观点，无法捕捉真实辩论场景中所需的多样性与说服力，限制了批判性思维和平衡对话的效果。

Method: 提出PTCG（Persona-guided Tree-based Counterargument Generation）框架，核心包括两部分：(1) 从原始论点中估计作者的说话人个性；(2) 融合代表不同视角的说话人个性，并结合受Tree-of-Thoughts启发的逐步生成与剪枝策略，实现个性引导的多样化反驳论点生成。

Result: 通过LLM-as-a-Judge评估、基于分类器的评估以及人工评估，PTCG在反驳论点的多样性和说服力两个维度上相较于基线方法均取得一致的提升。

Conclusion: 个性引导结合树状搜索生成能够有效弥补现有方法在反驳论点多样性上的不足，为批判性思维和平衡辩论场景中的自动反驳生成提供了可行且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The ability to generate counterarguments is important for critical thinking and balanced discourse, yet existing approaches typically produce only a single counterargument, failing to capture the diversity and persuasiveness required in real-world debates. To address this limitation, we propose Persona-guided Tree-based Counterargument Generation (PTCG), a framework that combines Tree-of-Thoughts-inspired step-wise generation and pruning with speaker persona selection. By estimating the author's persona from the original argument and incorporating speaker personas representing distinct perspectives, PTCG operationalizes perspective-taking and enables the generation of diverse counterarguments. Results from LLM-as-a-Judge, classifier-based assessment, and human evaluations indicate that PTCG shows consistent improvements in both the diversity and persuasiveness of counterarguments compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [170] [Line-Coupled Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07129)
*Shiyuan Li,Shaorong Zhang,Zhaorui Yang,Qian Zhang,Greg Ver Steeg,Bingyao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出行耦合语言模型（LCLM），一个自回归模型，通过同时推进多个文本行来增加每次前向传播生成的token数量。LCLM将各行的token交错排列为单一因果序列，并通过共享因果上下文耦合各行，同时保持标准的next-token目标和因果注意力。实验表明，跨行目标间的依赖度显著低于同行连续目标，使行成为可行的并行生成单元。881M参数下，LCLM每次前向传播平均生成2.94个内容token（对比基线1.00个），验证交叉熵损失2.44（对比基线2.39）；即使每步生成16个token，损失也只比基线高0.09（2.34 vs 2.25）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型每次解码步骤仅生成一个token，限制了每次前向传播的有效产出。虽然扩散模型、插入式解码和多token预测等方法支持并行生成，但要么增加了训练时的token流量开销，要么难以预测强依赖的未来token。作者希望设计一种既能保持标准自回归训练目标，又能实现并行生成多个token的模型架构。

Method: LCLM（Line-Coupled Language Model）的核心方法：(1) 自回归地同时推进多个文本行，每条活跃行都预测其下一token；(2) 将不同行的token交错排列为单一因果序列，并通过共享因果上下文将所有行耦合；(3) 使用行交错（line-staggered）旋转位置编码；(4) 保留标准的next-token训练目标和因果注意力机制，无需额外训练开销。

Result: 受控实验证实跨行目标间的相互依赖明显低于同行连续目标。在881M参数规模下，LCLM每次前向传播平均产出2.94个内容token，验证交叉熵损失为2.44，而普通自回归基线仅生成1.00个token/步，损失2.39。当LCLM每次前向传播生成16个token时，其损失仅比基线高0.09（2.34 vs 2.25）。

Conclusion: LCLM通过在行的粒度上进行并行自回归生成，显著提升了每次前向传播的token产出效率（约3倍于普通自回归模型），同时几乎不损失生成质量，验证了"行"作为并行生成单元的可行性，为高效文本生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models generate one token per decoding step, limiting the useful output of each forward pass. Although diffusion models, insertion-based decoding, and multi-token prediction enable parallel generation, they either incur additional training-time token traffic or struggle to predict strongly dependent future tokens. We introduce the Line-Coupled Language Model (LCLM), an autoregressive model that advances multiple text lines together by predicting the next token for every active line while coupling the lines through shared causal context. LCLM interleaves line tokens into a single causal sequence and uses line-staggered rotary positions, retaining the standard next-token objective and causal attention. Controlled experiments show that cross-line targets are substantially less dependent than consecutive same-line targets, supporting lines as parallel generation units. With 881M parameters, LCLM produces an average of 2.94 content tokens per forward pass with a validation cross-entropy loss of 2.44, compared with 1.00 token per forward pass and a loss of 2.39 for the vanilla autoregressive baseline. Most notably, even when LCLM generates 16 tokens per forward pass, its loss is only 0.09 higher than that of the vanilla autoregressive baseline (2.34 vs. 2.25).

</details>


### [171] [Retrieval-Augmented Multi-Prompt Ensemble for Minor-Grain Breeding Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07134)
*Hang Zhao,Jiahao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RAME（检索增强多提示集成），一种无需训练的框架，用于CCL2026-Eval任务5小杂粮育种信息抽取（MGBIE）文献联合抽取12类实体和6类关系。RAME结合混合BM25-嵌入检索、三种提示（Strict/Relaxed/Balanced）集成及大规模重复采样+多数投票，基于DeepSeek-V4-Flash取得总分0.499（NER 0.730，RE 0.346），排名第1，较官方GPT-5.5基线相对提升11.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对小杂粮育种文献中细粒度信息抽取的联合抽取任务（包括12种实体与6种关系的共同提取），旨在无需额外训练的前提下提升大模型在此专业领域上的抽取精度与置信度。

Method: 提出RAME（Retrieval-Augmented Multi-Prompt Ensemble）框架：通过混合BM25与嵌入检索实现检索增强的少样本选择；利用三种提示策略（Strict、Relaxed、Balanced）覆盖精确率到召回率的谱系；结合大规模重复采样与多数投票聚合筛选高置信度预测，全程无需训练，基于DeepSeek-V4-Flash模型。

Result: 在公开排行榜上RAME达到总分0.499（NER 0.730，RE 0.346），排名第1，超出由GPT-5.5驱动的官方Track-A基线（0.448），实现11.4%的相对提升。

Conclusion: RAME证明了无需训练、仅靠检索增强与多提示集成即能在专业领域的联合命名实体识别与关系抽取上超越强大的通用大模型基线，验证了多数投票聚合在提升预测置信度上的有效性，并公开了代码。

Abstract: This paper presents our system for CCL2026-Eval Task 5: Minor-Grain Breeding Information Extraction (MGBIE), which jointly extracts 12 entity types and 6 relation types from minor-grain breeding literature. We propose RAME (Retrieval-Augmented Multi-Prompt Ensemble), a training-free framework that elicits multiple LLM outputs under controlled diversity and aggregates them by majority voting to obtain high-confidence predictions. RAME combines (i) retrieval-augmented few-shot selection via a hybrid BM25-embedding retriever, (ii) a three-prompt ensemble (Strict, Relaxed, Balanced) spanning the precision to recall spectrum, and (iii) large-scale repeated sampling with majority voting to filter noisy predictions. Built on DeepSeek-V4-Flash, RAME achieves a Total Score of 0.499 (NER 0.730, RE 0.346) on the leaderboard, ranking 1st and surpassing the official Track-A baseline powered by GPT-5.5 (0.448), representing an 11.4% relative improvement. Code is available at https://github.com/king-wang123/CCL26-RAME.

</details>


### [172] [Vishing-Tactics-Bench: Forecasting Exploitation Trajectories in Voice Phishing Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07151)
*Jeongmin Lee,Dongmyung Sul,Seung Yun,Jinxia Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Vishing-Tactics-Bench基准，将从MITRE ATT&CK改编的6战术分类法（Vishing-Tactics）应用于语音钓鱼（vishing）防御，将事后欺诈分类转变为实时危害预测（Information Gathering或Financial Exploitation）。基于Endsley情境感知框架，标注了5,645个合成中文电话中的35,340条诈欺者话语，并定义了生存式协议的Exploitation Trajectory Forecasting任务及三个评估指标（AP@k, C-index, divergence error）。实验表明战术轨迹可作为对话战术状态的可解释表征，支持面向特定危害的预测及下游干预选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音钓鱼（vishing）是实时进行的，事后分类时危害已发生；需要更具操作性的问题是在通话进行中预测通话正趋向哪种具体危害（信息收集或金融剥削），以支持实时干预。

Method: 基于Endsley情境感知框架构建vishing防御为危害预测任务；将MITRE ATT&CK适配为6战术分类法（Vishing-Tactics）；标注35,340条诈欺者话语（5,645个合成中文电话）；定义Exploitation Trajectory Forecasting生存式协议，使用AP@k、C-index和divergence error三个指标评估；baseline涵盖从马尔可夫启发式到微调LLM。

Result: 各类baseline（马尔可夫启发式到微调LLM）表明战术轨迹能作为对话战术状态的可解释表征，支持面向特定危害的预测；分层提前时间分析在严格误报预算下识别出通话中战术轨迹信号可产生早期预警的时间点。

Conclusion: 战术轨迹为vishing的实时危害预测提供了可解释且有效的表征，可用于下游干预选择，实现通话中即时的早期预警。

Abstract: Voice phishing (vishing) unfolds in real time; by the time a call has ended and post-hoc classification is possible, the harm has already been done. The more actionable question is which concrete harm (Information Gathering or Financial Exploitation) an ongoing call is tactically progressing toward. We present Vishing-Tactics-Bench, a benchmark grounded in Endsley's situation-awareness (SA) framework that recasts vishing defense from after-the-fact fraud classification to harm projection: predicting at each turn whether the call will reach either terminal harm. We adapt MITRE ATT&CK to vishing as a 6-tactic taxonomy (Vishing-Tactics) and label 35,340 scammer utterances across 5,645 synthetic Chinese calls. We define Exploitation Trajectory Forecasting, a survival-style protocol over the two terminal harms with three metrics: AP@k, C-index, and divergence error. Baselines ranging from a Markov heuristic to fine-tuned LLMs show that the tactical trajectory serves as an interpretable representation of the call's tactical state, supporting harm-specific forecasting, which can then be used for the downstream application of intervention selection; a stratified lead-time analysis at a tight false-alarm budget further identifies at what point in a call the trajectory signal yields early warning.

</details>


### [173] [FreqBLiMP: Frequency-Controlled Minimal Pairs Reveal Robustness and Fragility of LLMs Under Lexical Rarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07153)
*Tyrone White,Yuki Arase*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FreqBLiMP，一个频率受控的BLiMP扩展基准，用于系统考察词汇频率对语言模型语法判断能力的影响。研究发现降低词汇频率会一贯地降低句子似然度，但整体对比可接受性准确率仅小幅下降；然而这种整体稳定性掩盖了不同语言现象间的显著差异——模型在显性形态句法泛化上保持稳健，但在依赖词元特定信息的现象上表现退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最小对基准（如BLiMP）主要评估语言模型在可接受与不可接受句子之间的语法偏好，但忽略了词汇频率这一自然语言中普遍存在且高度偏斜（Zipf分布）的特性。因此，现有评估无法检验语法偏好是否在涉及稀有词汇时保持稳定。

Method: 作者构建FreqBLiMP，在保持每个最小对的语法对比的前提下，对所有67个范式在显式Zipf频率机制下重新生成测试数据，从而控制词汇频率变量。随后在多个不同规模的开源权重LLM家族上进行系统评估。

Result: 研究发现：降低词汇频率会导致句子似然度的一致性单调下降，但整体对比可接受性准确率仅适度降低。然而，这种总体稳定性掩盖了不同语言现象间的较大差异：LLM在显性形态句法泛化上保持鲁棒，而在需要词元特定语言信息（lemma-specific information）的现象上则出现性能退化。

Conclusion: FreqBLiMP揭示了语法判断在稀有词汇条件下存在现象层面的非均匀退化，说明现有基准可能高估了LM对罕见词汇情境的语法泛化能力，尤其在依赖词汇特异知识的语法现象上需要更细致的模型评估。

Abstract: Minimal-pair benchmarks such as BLiMP evaluate linguistic knowledge by testing whether language models (LMs) prefer acceptable sentences over minimally different unacceptable ones. However, these benchmarks largely ignore lexical frequency variation, despite lexical frequency being a pervasive and highly skewed property of natural language use. Consequently, existing evaluations do not test whether grammatical preferences remain stable when contrasts involve rare lexical items. We introduce FreqBLiMP, a frequency-controlled extension of BLiMP that regenerates all 67 paradigms under explicit Zipf-frequency regimes while preserving each minimal-pair's grammatical contrast. Evaluating multiple open-weight LLM families across scales, we find that decreasing lexical frequency produces a consistent, monotonic decrease in sentence likelihood, but only a modest reduction in overall contrastive acceptability accuracy. However, this aggregate stability masks substantial variation across linguistic phenomena, with LLMs remaining robust on overt morphosyntactic generalization while degrading on phenomena that require lemma-specific information.

</details>


### [174] [In-Place Instruction Following in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07160)
*Zheng Nie,Zherui Li,Jiaming Zhang,Kun Wang,Zhenhong Zhou,Yufei Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文正式定义了In-place Instruction Following (IIF)任务并构建IIF-Bench层次化基准，包含字面、风格和语篇功能约束及基于rubric的局部-全局评估协议。通过注意力偏置探针发现vanilla dLLMs在去噪过程中对约束区间关注不足，进而提出GRAFT框架（约束感知SFT+偏好优化），在四个代表性dLLMs上将平均IIF分数从57.75提升至73.10（+15.35分），同时保持通用生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）通过双向迭代去噪生成文本，天然支持在任意输出位置锚定用户指定约束（In-place Prompting范式），但该能力缺乏系统形式化定义、基准测试以及专门训练方法。

Method: 1) 形式化定义IIF任务并构建分层基准IIF-Bench（涵盖字面、风格、语篇功能三类约束）；2) 设计基于rubric的局部-全局两级评估协议；3) 通过推理时注意力偏置探针诊断vanilla dLLMs对约束区间关注不足的问题；4) 提出GRAFT——面向IPP的后训练框架，结合约束感知监督微调（SFT）与偏好优化。

Result: 在四种代表性dLLMs上，GRAFT将平均IIF分数从57.75提升至73.10（+15.35分），字面约束和语篇功能约束上分别取得15.91和15.57分的绝对提升，同时保持通用文本生成能力不下降。

Conclusion: GRAFT框架能够有效增强dLLMs就地约束遵循能力（即IIF），验证了面向IPP范式的约束感知后训练方法的有效性，为该方向的研究提供了标准化的任务定义、评测基准和改进方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) generate text via bidirectional iterative denoising, naturally supporting user-specified constraints anchored at arbitrary output positions, a paradigm known as In-place Prompting (IPP). We formalize this as the In-place Instruction Following (IIF) task and construct IIF-Bench, a hierarchical benchmark spanning literal, style, and discourse-function constraints, paired with a rubric-based local-global evaluation protocol. An inference-time attention-bias probe suggests that vanilla dLLMs often under-prioritize constraint spans during denoising. We then propose GRAFT, an IPP-oriented post-training framework combining constraint-aware SFT and preference optimization. On four representative dLLMs, GRAFT raises the average IIF score from 57.75 to 73.10 (+15.35 points), with absolute gains of 15.91 and 15.57 points on literal and discourse-function constraints, while preserving general generation ability.

</details>


### [175] [CircuitLens: Reasoning Circuits as Data Selection Signals for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07183)
*Zhuofan Chen,Ziqian Jiao,Yikai Cui,Zhixin Cai,Jun Bai,Wenge Rong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出电路推理得分（CRS），一种基于冻结基础模型中46个推理敏感注意力头的对比消融信号，用于RLVR数据选择。实验发现低电路参与度的数据反而在中等难度基准上表现更好，且数据选择呈现场景依赖性而非静态质量排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR的训练效果对训练数据的选择高度敏感，但现有选择标准（难度过滤、人工策展、奖励轨迹评分）都将数据价值视为与学习模型无关的固有属性，忽略了数据价值与特定目标模型之间的关系。

Method: 通过对比消融识别出46个对推理敏感的注意力头，在冻结基础模型上单次前向传播即可计算得到CRS分数，无需奖励标签或rollout。CRS基于更强调推理电路参与度会产生更好训练数据的假设，测试不同电路参与度分组的数据在RLVR中的效果。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-7B上，最低电路参与度十分位数据优于随机选择（GSM8K +2.0pp、OlympiadBench +1.6pp、Minerva +2.9pp），而高参与度十分位增益更小且与中间十分位无显著差异。但该优势存在边界条件：领域策展数据池中无方法能区分彼此；1.5B规模下有利方向不同；最低奖励训练条件下下游泛化最强。

Conclusion: 在Qwen2.5-Math设置下，RLVR数据选择是场景依赖的，不能简化为问题的静态质量排序。低电路参与度数据反而更有价值的反直觉发现挑战了"更强推理电路参与产生更好训练数据"的直觉假设。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is sensitive to which problems a model trains on, yet existing selection criteria--difficulty filtering, hand-curation, reward-trajectory scoring--assess data value as an intrinsic property of problems, independent of the model that will learn from them. We introduce Circuit Reasoning Score (CRS), a selection signal derived from 46 reasoning-sensitive attention heads identified via contrastive ablation, computed in a single forward pass on the frozen base model without reward labels or rollouts. CRS runs against the intuitive hypothesis that stronger reasoning-circuit engagement produces better training data: on Qwen2.5-Math-7B, the lowest-engagement decile improves over random selection on three medium-difficulty benchmarks (GSM8K +2.0 pp, OlympiadBench +1.6 pp, Minerva +2.9 pp), while the highest-engagement decile gains less and is indistinguishable from the middle decile. The advantage has boundary conditions: on a domain-curated pool no selection method separates from the others; at 1.5B scale the useful direction differs; and the lowest-reward training condition produces the strongest downstream generalization. Within the Qwen2.5-Math settings tested, RLVR data selection appears regime-dependent rather than reducible to a static ranking of problem quality.

</details>


### [176] [SIFTING: A Novel LLM-Based Framework for Structured and Transparent Information Extraction from Clinical Free-Text Reports, with Application to Tumor Staging in Lung Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07185)
*Mirco Hess,Gerben van Veenendaal,Joris Wakkie,Yiwen Soo,Malcolm H. Lawson,John D. Maclay,Arjun Nair,Neal Navani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SIFTING框架，一个基于LLM的临床自由文本信息提取系统，它结合了分段处理、结构化输出和严格输出控制，通过将提取结果链接到源文本来实现可追溯性。在130份肺癌影像报告中提取T分期的任务中达到90%的准确率，与大型前沿LLM相当，同时提供完全的数据控制和可验证输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在临床自由文本信息提取方面展现潜力，但存在两个关键缺陷：输出通常是非结构化的，且缺乏可追溯性（即无法追溯到源文本），这使得验证困难并阻碍了临床工作流中的实际应用。

Method: SIFTING框架结合了LLM的语言理解能力与分段级别处理、结构化提示词和严格输出控制。将提取结果与源文本片段关联以实现准确且透明的信息提取。在概念验证中，部署了全自托管的Llama-3.3-70B（4-bit量化版，35GB），应用于130份肺癌影像报告中的T分期提取任务（SIFTING-T-stage），并与四位临床专家构建的参考标准及多种LLM的单提示词方法进行比较，使用bootstrap重抽样估算置信区间。

Result: SIFTING-T-stage在参考标准上达到90%的准确率（95% CI: 84-95）。性能与最先进的大型推理型LLM相当，且与临床专家具有可互换性（p < 0.001），同时通过源文本引用提供完整可追溯性。

Conclusion: SIFTING实现了从临床自由文本中准确、结构化且可追溯的信息提取。它确保数据控制、可复现性和可验证的输出，能够支持临床验证和工作流集成。

Abstract: Background: Large language models (LLMs) show promise for extracting information from clinical free-text documents, but their outputs are often unstructured and lack traceability, complicating validation and adoption in clinical workflows. In this work we introduce SIFTING, an LLM-based framework designed to address these shortcomings.
  Methods: SIFTING combines the language comprehension capabilities of LLMs with segment-level processing and structured prompts with strict output control, linking findings to the source text to enable both accurate and transparent information extraction. To demonstrate its capabilities, we applied the framework to the task of extracting tumor T-stage information from 130 lung cancer radiology reports (SIFTING-T-stage). A compact 4-bit quantized version of the open-source LLM Llama-3.3-70B (35 GB) was used in a fully self-hosted setup, providing full control over data and model. Performance was evaluated against a reference standard created by four clinical experts and compared with a range of LLMs as used in a conventional single-prompt approach, using bootstrap resampling to estimate confidence intervals.
  Results: SIFTING-T-stage achieved an accuracy of 90% (95% CI: 84-95) against the reference standard. We found its performance to be comparable to even the largest state-of-the-art LLMs with reasoning capabilities and to be interchangeable with clinical experts (p < 0.001), while at the same time offering full traceability through source text references.
  Conclusion: SIFTING enables accurate, structured, and traceable information extraction from clinical free-text documents. It ensures data control, reproducibility, and verifiable outputs that can support clinical validation and workflow integration.

</details>


### [177] [CEDAR: Error-Bounded Residual Routing for Efficient Long-Context Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07237)
*Siyu Li,Dong Wang,Jie Zhou,Wei Li,Yang Xu,Sijie Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CEDAR是一种粗到细的注意力路由方法，通过残差摘要路径保持全局覆盖，仅对高误差语义块展开精确token注意力，以在线性softmax归一化中替代而非复制粗证据，在128K长上下文场景下恢复硬稀疏路由丢失的大部分质量并保持约3倍核加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后验稀疏注意力（hard selection）对省略的块分配零概率，路由失误不可恢复，且固定扩展预算对简单与模糊查询花费相同计算量，缺乏自适应性和误差感知能力。

Method: CEDAR采用粗到细策略：每个语义块提供廉价的key-value摘要加入残差注意力路径；估算近似误差高的块再被展开为精确token注意力，精确与摘要贡献在单一softmax归一化中合并，使精细化替代而非复制粗证据。同时推导基于块内key/value离散度的输出误差界，用于分配可变的精细化预算。

Result: 在受控的聚类注意力研究中，残差摘要相比硬丢弃在相同精确块预算下将重建误差降低超过98%；长上下文基准实验表明CEDAR恢复了硬稀疏路由丢失的大部分质量，同时在128K上下文下保持约3倍核加速。

Conclusion: CEDAR通过误差感知的粗到细动态路由和残差归一化机制，在保持模型冻结且维持全局覆盖的前提下，有效解决了硬稀疏路由的不可恢复性和计算分配不均问题，实现了质量与效率的良好权衡。

Abstract: Post-hoc sparse attention accelerates long-context prefill by routing each query to a small set of token-level interactions. Hard selection, however, assigns zero probability to every omitted chunk: a routing miss cannot be recovered, and a fixed expansion budget spends the same work on easy and ambiguous queries. We introduce Coarse-to-fine Error-aware Dynamic Attention Routing (CEDAR), a coarse-to-fine method that keeps the language model frozen while preserving global coverage. Each semantic chunk contributes a cheap key--value summary to a residual attention path; chunks with high estimated approximation error are then expanded to exact token attention. Exact and summarized contributions are combined in a single softmax normalization, so refinement replaces, rather than duplicates, coarse evidence. We derive an output-error bound governed by within-chunk key/value dispersion and use it to allocate a variable refinement budget. A controlled clustered-attention study shows that residual summaries reduce reconstruction error by more than 98% relative to hard dropping at equal exact-chunk budgets. Experiments on long-context benchmarks demonstrate that CEDAR recovers most of the quality lost by hard sparse routing while maintaining approximately $3\times$ kernel speedup at 128K context.

</details>


### [178] [Separating Stream Stability from Long-Term Recall in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07282)
*Peipei Cao,Xin Zhang,Jie Tang,Xiao Li,Siying Li,Qing Pei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出应区分"流式语言模型的稳定性"与"长上下文/记忆能力"两个问题，引入稳定性、访问性、效用三个时间跨度（horizon）来刻画模型在无限流上的行为，并提出ThreeH评估框架，在统一状态与计算预算下测量三个跨度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献常将流式语言模型与长上下文及记忆系统混为一谈，但attention sink等流式方法虽然能实现无限长的稳定自回归生成，却无法利用已离开近期缓存的内容。作者认为应在系统声明与评测中明确这一区别，以避免对模型能力的过度宣称。

Method: 引入三个时间跨度概念：稳定性跨度（行为稳定可预测的范围）、可访问跨度（过去内容仍能因果影响输出的范围）、效用跨度（任务仍保持可接受性能的范围）。在此基础上提出ThreeH评估契约，在统一状态与计算预算约束下同时测量三种跨度，并构造性证明稳定性跨度可为无限而另外两者有限。

Result: 在128K token流、延迟绑定回忆与延迟决策等实验上表明：attention sink能保持恒定内存与稳定的本地建模，但不能作为语义记忆访问超出活动缓存的内容；而循环状态与检索式状态可扩展语义跨度。ThreeH框架厘清了缓存策略、循环状态、检索与外部记忆各自的作用。

Conclusion: 流式生成稳定性不应被误当作记忆能力。通过显式区分稳定性、访问性与效用三个跨度，ThreeH提供了一个更严格且可操作的评估契约，有助于对流式语言模型及其记忆扩展方法进行准确、公平的比较。

Abstract: Methods for streaming language models are often discussed alongside long-context and memory systems, although they solve different problems. An attention sink can stabilize autoregressive generation over an indefinitely long stream while the model remains unable to use content that has left its recent-token cache. We argue that this distinction should be explicit in system claims and evaluation. We introduce three horizons: the stability horizon, over which predictive behavior remains well behaved; the access horizon, over which past content can still causally affect the output; and the utility horizon, over which a task retains acceptable performance. We show constructively that the stability horizon can be infinite while the access and utility horizons are finite. We then propose ThreeH, an evaluation contract that measures all three horizons under a common state and compute budget. Applying the framework to attention-sink streaming clarifies its strength, constant-memory, stable generation, without treating anchor tokens as semantic memory. The framework exposes roles for cache policies, recurrent state, retrieval, and external memory. Experiments on 128K-token streams, delayed binding recall, and delayed decisions show that attention sinks preserve local modeling but not content beyond the active cache; recurrent and retrieval state extend the semantic horizon.

</details>


### [179] [Probing the Structure and Dynamics of LLM Value Expression through Value Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07296)
*Kaicheng Zhang,Jingyi Xiao,Renjun Hu,Xiaoling Liu,Yunshi Lan,Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出冲突驱动的价值探测框架（Conflict-driven Value Probing），通过对LLM施加价值冲突并实施四种干预扰动，揭示LLM价值表达具有结构化但动态的特性，识别出表达双重性、功能可导引性和有限可塑性三种模式，说明上下文能在行为边界内灵活重配置表达的价值优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM的伦理评估通常将模型价值视为静态、单一的，作者认为这种观点忽略了价值表达的结构性与动态性，主张应将其理解为一种结构化但动态的现象。

Method: 引入Conflict-driven Value Probing框架：将LLM置于价值冲突情境中，并实施四类干预来扰动冲突以探测价值表达；该框架应用于十个LLM。

Result: 识别出三个重复出现的模式：(1)表达双重性——模型在抽象评估中偏向理想化取向，在具体冲突中转向务实优先级；(2)功能可导引性——模型能按任务定义的价值目标轻易重配置价值表达；(3)有限可塑性——重配置受到约束，压力会引发安全和目标导向的优先级转移，负面框架则能区分受保护价值与可被重定向的价值。

Conclusion: LLM价值表达具有结构与动态双重特征：上下文能灵活重配置表达的价值优先级，但存在于行为边界之内。该行为学解释为理解LLM的可控性、对齐与安全性提供了基础。

Abstract: Ethical evaluation of Large Language Models (LLMs) often characterizes model values as static and monolithic. In contrast, we argue that LLM value expression is better understood as a structured yet dynamic phenomenon. To investigate this, we introduce Conflict-driven Value Probing, a controlled framework that places LLMs in value conflicts and implements four types of interventions that perturb these conflicts to probe LLM value expression. Applying this framework to ten LLMs, we identify three recurring patterns. (1) Expression duality: models shift from broad idealistic orientations in abstract assessment toward more pragmatic priorities in concrete conflicts. (2) Functional steerability: models readily reconfigure their expressed value profiles toward task-defined value objectives. (3) Bounded plasticity: such reconfiguration is not without constraints, i.e. pressure induces a security- and goal-oriented priority shift while negative framing distinguishes protected values from those more amenable to redirection. Together, these findings characterize both the structure and dynamics of LLM value expression: context flexibly reconfigures expressed priorities, yet within behavioral boundaries. This behavioral account provides a foundation for understanding controllability, alignment, and safety in LLMs. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ZeroGen-Lab/CFProbe.

</details>


### [180] [Marginal Fidelity Does Not Establish User Simulation in Demographic Synthetic Survey Panels: Response Contracts, Support Collapse and Conditioning Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07305)
*Alexander Doudkin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文检验了"汇总边际匹配"作为人口学合成调查面板验证标准所建立的事实范畴。研究发现，该验证主要反映的是"回答契约"与估计目标的性质，而非个体级仿真的真实性。具体而言：多选题中"选出全部适用项"的承诺式作答会使大量选项槽留空，而逐选项概率引出可将边际MAE降低4.53~7.30点（有上限的问卷需先投影到其最大值才出现反转）；同时，无人物设定的直接总体-流行率查询在九个对齐模型-电池对上平均MAE为6.27，优于承诺面板的12.39并在全部九次对比中胜出，说明边际一致不能区分"模拟应答者"与"直接总体估计"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人口学综合样本面板(synthetic panels)常以"与已发表调查的汇总答案匹配"作为验证凭据，但这一凭据究竟证明了什么尚不明确。作者希望厘清：当合成面板与人类目标仅在总体边际上一致时，这种一致性究竟是(1)应答契约/引出方式造成的人为现象，(2)可用无仿真直接估计的总体参数，还是(3)对个体级模拟真实性的真正证据。若无法区分这三者，则该验证标准在方法论上存在根本缺陷，需要在3个国家、4个调查机构、6个多选电池上系统检验。

Method: 使用来自三个国家四个调查机构的六个多选电池(multiselect batteries)进行系统对比。将拟合分析限制在三个"综合队列与人类目标共享声明总体框架"的工具上，其余三个电池作为敏感性分析。核心实验设计包括：(1)承诺式集合(committed sets)与逐选项概率引出(per-option probability elicitation)两种回答契约的对比；(2)有上限(capped)与无限模型-工具比较中的边际误差分析；(3)引入一个"无人设的总体-流行率查询"(no-persona population-prevalence query)作为基准，与承诺面板和约束感知概率向量(约5.34 MAE)在9个对齐模型-电池对上进行对抗性比较，并在3个未发表的人口单元格上验证。

Result: (a) 回答契约主导所测保真度：对齐工具中，600名应答者的面板里承诺式集合在128个模型-电池选项槽中留下66个为空，而逐选项概率引出为0/128；(b) 在8个不限容的模型-工具对比中，概率将选项边际MAE降低4.53至7.30点；封顶工具需将向量投影到其声明的最大值后才在两个模型上出现反转；(c) 这些是测量效应——人类目标为现实中"全选适用项"的作答，向量则是潜在包含倾向；(d) 无人设的总体流行率查询平均MAE 6.27 vs 承诺面板12.39，在全部9个对齐对中胜出；(e) 约束感知概率向量平均5.34并在9中4胜过基准；(f) 在3个未发表人口单元格上，两种方法都无法胜过简单地复述全国分布。

Conclusion: 总体边际一致性所构成的验证证据是关于"引出契约"和"无需模拟受访者即可获得的估计量"的证据，而非关于个体级仿真的证据。边际一致性无法区分个体仿真与直接总体估计，从而对当前将"匹配公开调查边际"作为综合面板有效性凭据的做法提出根本性质疑。除非验证标准能针对期望的估计目标(个体模拟的真实性)而非总体参数，否则该验证在逻辑上是循环的或无辨识力的。

Abstract: Demographic synthetic survey panels are often validated by matching aggregate answers to published surveys. We test what that certificate establishes across six multiselect batteries from four survey organisations in three countries. The headline analysis is restricted to three instruments whose synthetic cohort and human target share the stated population frame; three other batteries remain sensitivity analyses.
  The response contract dominates measured fidelity. In the aligned instruments, committed sets leave 66 of 128 model-battery option slots empty in panels of up to 500 respondents, versus 0 of 128 under per-option probability elicitation. Across eight uncapped model-instrument comparisons, probabilities reduce option-marginal MAE by 4.53 to 7.30 points. The capped instrument reverses on two models until the vectors are projected onto its stated maximum. These are measurement effects: human targets are realised check-all responses, whereas the vectors are latent inclusion propensities.
  Published marginal agreement also fails to discriminate respondent simulation from direct population estimation. On nine aligned model-battery pairs, a no-persona population-prevalence query averages 6.27 MAE versus 12.39 for committed panels and wins all nine comparisons. Constraint-aware probability vectors average 5.34 and beat the query on four of nine, so the baseline challenges the validation criterion rather than proving direct estimation uniformly best. On three unpublished demographic cells, neither approach beats reciting the national distribution. Population-marginal agreement is therefore evidence about an elicitation contract and an estimand obtainable without simulated respondents, not evidence of individual simulation.

</details>


### [181] [RouteRelay: Event-Triggered Cross-Layer Route Reuse for Efficient Dynamic Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07306)
*Bin Li,Sisi Liu,Chenyang Hu,Chaoyang Zhang,Wei Li,Hui Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RouteRelay是一种router无关的方法，通过跨层复用路由元数据来降低动态稀疏注意力中路由器重建chunk-chunk分数矩阵的开销，在几乎不损失路由召回率的情况下大幅减少分数计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态稀疏注意力在每层Transformer中都需要路由器重建chunk级分数矩阵来选择top-k key chunks，即使跨层选取的路由变化很小。这种逐层重复的全量路由计算带来了显著的预填充成本，而相邻层之间路由的高度相似性未被利用。

Method: RouteRelay通过锚点层执行完整路由，而中间层仅对前一层的top-k路由进行重打分，并引入一个包含near-miss和随机探测chunk的紧凑哨兵集合。只有当哨兵挑战了查询行最弱的已选chunk时才会重新路由该行。论文还给出了top-k稳定性条件、挑战者遗漏的概率界，以及逐行选择的GPU执行设计。

Result: 在低、中、高跨层漂移下分别只需重新路由25.0%、55.4%和78.2%的行即可保持至少99.99%的路由召回率。跨路由规模实验（key chunk数从128增至1024）中，仅需评估完整路由38.4%-51.6%的分数对即可保持100%召回率。不过其未融合的CPU执行仍慢于稠密矩阵乘法，行压缩与ledger更新是主要内核工程优化目标。

Conclusion: RouteRelay证明了跨层路由元数据复用能显著降低动态稀疏注意力的路由开销，且具有理论保障路由质量；但其实际性能提升受限于内核层面的工程实现，CPU未融合实现尚不及稠密矩阵乘法。

Abstract: Dynamic sparse attention reduces long-context prefill cost by routing each query chunk to a small set of key chunks at every Transformer layer. The sparse attention kernel avoids most token interactions, but the router still rebuilds a chunk--chunk score matrix layer after layer, even when the selected routes change little. We introduce RouteRelay, a router-agnostic method that reuses only route metadata across depth while continuing to compute attention with the current layer's queries, keys, and values. Anchor layers perform full routing. Intermediate layers rescore the previous top-$k$ route and a compact sentinel set of near-miss and randomly probed chunks. A query row is rerouted only when a sentinel challenges its weakest selected chunk. We give a top-$k$ stability condition, a probabilistic bound on missed challengers, and a row-selective GPU execution design. In a reproducible empirical evaluation, RouteRelay retains at least 99.99% route recall while rerouting 25.0%, 55.4%, and 78.2% of rows under low, moderate, and high cross-layer drift, respectively. Across routing scales, RouteRelay retains 100.0% recall while evaluating 38.4--51.6% of full-routing score pairs as the key-chunk count grows from 128 to 1024. Its unfused CPU execution remains slower than dense matrix multiplication, exposing row compaction and ledger updates as the main kernel-engineering targets.

</details>


### [182] [SPARROW: Scalable Taxonomy Induction via Structure-Preserving Partitioning and Constraint-Guided Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07307)
*Yirui Zhang,Yixuan Tang,Yandong Sun,Mong-Li Lee,Anthony Kum Hoe Tung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARROW框架，通过结构保持的谱划分和约束引导的增量融合，解决大规模概念集Taxonomy归纳中分治合并范式带来的结构碎片化和父节点错位两大失效模式，在多个大规模基准上取得最优全局结构质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的Taxonomy归纳方法在处理平面术语列表时随概念规模扩大而性能急剧下降，现有研究发现这不仅是上下文长度限制所致，更深层的原因在于层次推理中的结构性失败。因此需要针对大规模概念集的可扩展归纳方法。

Method: 采用分治-合并范式：(1)结构保持的谱划分（structure-preserving spectral partitioning），在将概念划分成小子集时保留块内层次连通性；(2)约束引导的增量融合（constraint-guided incremental fusion），将块级关系视为全局放置的结构约束而非地面真值，避免局部合理关系在全局层级中被错误放置。

Result: 在多个大规模基准上的实验表明，SPARROW在不同骨干模型（backbones）上均一致地取得了最强的全局结构质量。

Conclusion: SPARROW通过针对分治合并范式中两类结构性失效模式的设计，实现了大规模Taxonomy归纳的高质量可扩展归纳，并已开放代码。

Abstract: Taxonomy induction aims to organize concept sets into coherent hierarchical structures. Recent LLM-based methods can induce taxonomies directly from flat term lists, avoiding the need for corpora, but degrade sharply as concept sets scale up. We argue that this degradation stems not only from context length limitations, but also from structural failures in hierarchical reasoning. To address this, we adopt a divide-and-merge paradigm that partitions concepts into smaller subsets, induces local taxonomies, and merges them into a global hierarchy. However, we identify two structural failure modes inherent to this paradigm: Structural Fragmentation, where partitioning weakens local hierarchical signals, and Parent Displacement, where locally plausible relations are misplaced in the global hierarchy. To address both, we propose SPARROW, a scalable taxonomy induction framework that combines structure-preserving spectral partitioning to retain hierarchical connectivity within each block, and constraint-guided incremental fusion that treats block-level relations as structural constraints rather than ground truth for global placement. Experiments on large-scale benchmarks show that SPARROW consistently achieves the strongest global structural quality across backbones. The code is available at https://github.com/rebeccazyr/SPARROW.

</details>


### [183] [LANTERN: Language Model Assessment on Noisy and Transformed Tasks for Understanding Error and Robustness Nuances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07309)
*Vamsi Krishna Kodavali,Rituraj Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了大语言模型(LLM)对输入扰动(包括词错误率、字符重复/复制、选项修改、指令遵循变化)的鲁棒性。作者构建了一个包含多项选择题(MCQ)和指令遵循任务的合成与增强数据集，在不同规模(小/中/大)以及基础与指令微调版本的LLM上进行了大规模实验，量化了扰动条件下模型响应的变异性，揭示了与基线模型的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的鲁棒性评估仍是一个关键挑战，特别是在评估其对输入数据扰动的敏感性方面缺乏系统性的多维度研究。需要建立统一的评估框架来量化LLM在各类扰动条件下的稳定性。

Method: 1) 构建合成与增强数据集，覆盖MCQ数据集和指令遵循任务；2) 从四个维度引入扰动：词错误率、字符重复与复制、选项修改、指令遵循变异性；3) 在不同规模(小/中/大)和不同变体(基础/指令微调)的LLM上进行广泛实验；4) 量化模型在扰动条件下响应的变异性并与基线模型对比。

Result: 分析量化了模型在扰动条件下的响应变异性，并揭示了与基线模型相比的差异。研究发现了LLM在不同评估场景下稳定性的关键特征。

Conclusion: 该研究为LLM在各种评估场景下的稳定性提供了见解，有助于开发更鲁棒、更可靠的LLM，并推动更稳健的评估方法论建设。

Abstract: Robustness evaluation of large language models (LLMs) remains a critical challenge, particularly in assessing their sensitivity to perturbations in input data. In this work, we systematically evaluate LLM robustness across multiple dimensions, including word error rate, character repetition and duplication, modifications in choices, and variability in instruction following. To facilitate this evaluation, we construct a synthetic and augmented dataset encompassing a diverse set of LLM benchmarks, specifically targeting multiple-choice question (MCQ) datasets and instruction-following tasks. We conduct extensive experiments on LLMs of varying scales-small, medium, and large-as well as across base and instruction-tuned variants. Our analysis quantifies the variability in model responses under perturbed conditions and highlights discrepancies relative to baseline models. The findings provide insights into the stability of LLMs across different evaluation scenarios contributing to the development of more robust and reliable language models as well as robust evaluation methodologies.

</details>


### [184] [Content-Based Addressing for Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07314)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将令牌流划分为单元、单元内沿用RoPE、单元间用内容计算地址的新位置编码方法，将位置用于局部寻址、内容用于跨单元寻址，避免RoPE因上下文长度扩展而产生的训练-测试位置分布不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE依赖令牌的整数位置确定注意力中的旋转角度，当上下文长度增加时会产生训练与测试之间的位置不匹配。现有的缩放、插值、随机化或偏置等方法仍依赖增长的令牌计数器，本质上是扩展位置范围或标识表，而非真正解决未见过偏移的问题。

Method: 将令牌流划分为多个单元；在单元内部保留标准的RoPE位置；对每个已完成的单元，从其内容计算一个地址。新增单元时对新的内容应用已学习的映射而不是扩展位置范围或标识表。

Result: 理论上证明了该构造在单元内部精确保留RoPE、插入或重排单元时两个固定令牌间的注意力比较不变、且仅因增加单元不会产生新的相对旋转。实验中，Tiny Shakespeare字符级诊断中，使用256字符上下文的模型在256字符处验证困惑度为4.04，在4096处为3.82，而连续RoPE从4.71恶化到12.09。第二个诊断显示基于内容的寻址可以检索和利用多个序列化事实中的信息。

Conclusion: 尽管是受控的浅层实验而非大规模基准，结果支持直接的处方：用位置进行局部寻址，用内容进行跨单元的全局寻址。

Abstract: Rotary position embedding (RoPE) uses each token's integer position to determine the rotation applied inside attention. This works well for local token order, but increasing context length creates a positional train-test mismatch: RoPE produces relative rotations at offsets not seen during training. Methods that rescale, interpolate, randomize, or bias positions specify how attention handles those offsets, but still derive positional information from a growing token counter. We instead divide a token stream into units, retain ordinary RoPE positions within each unit, and assign every completed unit an address computed from its content. Adding units then applies the same learned map to new content rather than extending a positional range or an identifier table. We prove that this construction preserves local RoPE exactly, leaves the attention comparison between two fixed tokens unchanged when other units are inserted or reordered, and does not create new relative rotations merely because more units are added. In a character-level Tiny Shakespeare diagnostic, a model trained on 256-character contexts has validation perplexity 4.04 at 256 characters and 3.82 at 4096, while continuous RoPE changes from 4.71 to 12.09. A second diagnostic shows that content-based addressing can retrieve and use information from multiple serialized facts. These are controlled shallow experiments, not scale benchmarks, but they support a direct prescription: use position to address locally and content to address across units.

</details>


### [185] [BlueprintAgent: Constraint-Triggered Targeted Revisits for Simulation-Ready Generation from Scanned Structural Blueprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07362)
*Zhouyuan Xu,Chen Yang,Linhao Wang,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BlueprintAgent (BPA) 是一种面向扫描建筑蓝图的结构化框架提取多模态智能体，以MLLM为主导读取与决策者，OCR和计算机视觉提供局部证据，并通过将工程约束实现为可调用的验证器，在实体冲突时触发MLLM对局部区域做定向重审。在300张来自20个匿名RC框架项目的真实扫描图纸上，BPA实现Beam F1达0.994，远超单MLLM零样本的0.301和固定流水线的0.820。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将在役钢筋混凝土建筑蓝图转换为可用于模拟的结构化框架表示（支持确定性FEM导出与工程师复核）是安全评估和抗震加固的基础，但该过程目前依赖人工。直接对扫描图纸使用多模态大语言模型(MLLM)提示词生成结果不可靠，输出常违反梁柱支撑、跨数、三维连续性等工程约束，亟需一种将工程约束显式纳入推理过程的自动化方法。

Method: BPA将MLLM作为主要读取器和决策者，OCR与CV提供局部证据支持。核心机制是将工程约束实现为可调用的验证器：验证器返回实体级冲突报告，这些报告触发MLLM对局部区域进行有目标的重新审阅，从而实现推理时控制，区别于固定流水线和自由形式的自我反思。

Result: 在300张来自20个匿名RC框架项目的真实扫描图纸上评估，对比5个基线和6个消融实验。BPA达到宏平均Beam F1为0.994，而单MLLM零样本仅0.301、固定流水线为0.820。移除MLLM主导的轴线判定会在复杂多图项目中使Beam和Column F1显著下降。

Conclusion: 对于密集技术图纸，工程约束最适合作为触发实体级定向重审的条件，而非作为事后的输出过滤器。这一结论表明MLLM推理时的约束驱动纠错机制相比固定流水线或单纯后处理过滤更有效。

Abstract: Converting in-service reinforced-concrete (RC) building blueprints into simulation-ready models---structured frame representations that support deterministic FEM export and qualified-engineer review---underpins safety assessment and seismic retrofit, but the process remains manual. Direct prompting of a multimodal large language model (MLLM) over a scanned sheet is unreliable: outputs often violate engineering constraints on beam--column support, span count, or 3D continuity. We present BlueprintAgent (BPA), a constraint-triggered multimodal agent for simulation-ready frame extraction from scanned blueprints. BPA treats the MLLM as the primary reader and decision maker, with OCR and computer vision supplying localized evidence. Its central mechanism realizes engineering constraints as callable validators whose entity-level conflict reports trigger targeted MLLM revisits over the local region---an inference-time control distinct from fixed pipelines and free-form self-reflection. We evaluate BPA on 300 real scanned blueprint sheets from 20 anonymized RC frame projects, against five baselines and six ablations. BPA reaches a macro-averaged Beam F1 of 0.994, against 0.301 for single-MLLM zero-shot and 0.820 for a fixed pipeline; removing MLLM-led axis adjudication collapses Beam and Column F1 on complex multi-sheet projects. For dense technical drawings, engineering constraints are best deployed as triggers for entity-level targeted revisits rather than as post-hoc output filters.

</details>


### [186] [Beyond Fluent Generation: A CPU Reliability Benchmark for MCP-Style Tool Calling in Sub-2B Small Language Models for Edge Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07370)
*Abrar Shahriar Qurat-Ul-Ain Mastoi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了五个低于20亿参数的开源SLM（Phi-1.5、Pythia-1.4B、TinyLlama-1.1B-Chat、Qwen2.5-0.5B、Qwen2.5-1.5B）在CPU上执行MCP风格工具调用的能力。结果显示Qwen2.5-1.5B表现最佳（greedy 75%/sampling 79%），Qwen2.5-0.5B在sampling下性能骤降至32%，Phi-1.5得分为0%。1,000条原始响应中仅5条可直接解析为JSON，暴露了对输出恢复机制的严重依赖。资源探测显示Qwen2.5-1.5B需要7,960 MiB内存和30.782秒平均延迟，揭示边缘部署中存在可靠性-资源权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限的单板计算机（如树莓派、Jetson Nano等）推动在设备端部署SLM智能体，以减少云依赖、改善数据局部性并容忍间歇性连接。MCP风格的工具调用不仅需要流畅生成，还需要智能体输出机器可读的JSON、选择正确的工具并提供完整参数、避免意外操作。研究旨在建立平台无关的CPU基线，评估小模型在边缘设备上进行结构化工具调用的可行性。

Method: 选取五个低于20亿参数的开源模型（Phi-1.5、Pythia-1.4B、TinyLlama-1.1B-Chat、Qwen2.5-0.5B、Qwen2.5-1.5B），使用100个提示词（涵盖天气检索、网页搜索、计算、邮件撰写和任务创建）在greedy解码和nucleus采样下进行评估。引入恢复解析器（recovery parser）去除Markdown格式标记、提取花括号定界子串，并从可解析性、工具名正确性、参数完整性和值一致性四个维度评分。另设严格的事后审计和CPU资源探测以衡量原始可解析性和资源需求。

Result: Qwen2.5-1.5B在greedy下达到75%、sampling下达79%；Qwen2.5-0.5B在greedy下达72%但sampling下降至32%；Phi-1.5得分为0%；Pythia和TinyLlama最高仅达7%。严格事后审计仅发现1,000条原始响应中5条可直接解析为JSON。资源上Qwen2.5-1.5B需7,960 MiB内存和30.782秒平均延迟，Qwen2.5-0.5B需3,637 MiB和10.627秒。

Conclusion: 较小模型在边缘CPU上可用但存在显著可靠性-资源权衡：表现最好的模型需要更多资源，资源友好模型在采样解码下不稳定。研究局限在于未直接在命名板卡上测试，也未实现完整的MCP协议。安全部署需采用schema验证、受约束生成、最小权限执行以及对关键操作进行人工升级机制。

Abstract: Resource constrained single-board computers including Raspberry Pi, NVIDIA Jetson Nano, Arduino UNO Q, Orange Pi, and LattePanda motivate on-device small language model (SLM) agents that reduce cloud dependence, improve data locality, and tolerate intermittent connectivity. Model Context Protocol (MCP)-style tool invocation demands more than fluent generation: an agent must emit machine-readable JSON, select the correct tool, supply all required arguments, and avoid unintended actions. We establish a platform-agnostic CPU baseline by evaluating five open-weight models below two billion parameters Phi-1.5, Pythia-1.4B, TinyLlama-1.1B-Chat, Qwen2.5-0.5B, and Qwen2.5-1.5B on 100 prompts spanning weather retrieval, web search, calculation, email composition, and task creation, under greedy decoding and nucleus sampling. A recovery parser strips Markdown fences, extracts brace-delimited substrings, and scores parseability, tool-name correctness, argument completeness, and value agreement. Under this criterion, Qwen2.5-1.5B achieves 75% (greedy) and 79% (sampling); Qwen2.5-0.5B achieves 72% (greedy) but drops to 32% under sampling. Phi-1.5 scores 0%; Pythia and TinyLlama reach at most 7%. A strict post-hoc audit finds only 5 of 1,000 raw responses directly parseable as JSON, exposing near-total dependence on output recovery. A CPU resource probe shows Qwen2.5-1.5B requires 7,960 MiB and 30.782 s mean latency; Qwen2.5-0.5B uses 3,637 MiB and 10.627 s, revealing a reliability-resource trade-off for edge deployment. These results do not cover the named boards directly or a full MCP implementation. Safe deployment requires schema validation, constrained generation, least-privilege execution, and human escalation for consequential actions.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond Single-Negative Preference: Multi-Negative DPO for LLM-Centric Historical Entity Linking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07379)
*Tien Nam Nguyen,Emanuela Boros,Ahmed Hamdi,Adam Jatowt,Mickaël Coustaty,Antoine Doucet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多负样本直接偏好优化（MDPO）方法，将同一提及关联的所有有效负候选纳入偏好优化目标，用于大规模语言模型的历史实体链接任务。实验表明MDPO优于监督微调和单负样本DPO，在NIL提及、语义歧义、OCR噪声和历史困难名称等场景下提升尤为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型在历史实体链接中展现出潜力，但现有偏好优化方法在每次训练时仅使用一个负候选，忽略了检索到的其他负候选所包含的丰富信息，导致优化不充分。

Method: 提出MDPO（multi-negative direct preference optimisation），一种基于参考模型的成对目标函数，将正确实体与该提及关联的所有有效拒绝候选进行比较。MDPO保留DPO的Bradley-Terry公式形式，通过掩码和长度归一化的序列评分来利用完整候选集。

Result: 在hipe-2020和newseye数据集上评估（涵盖法语、德语、英语、瑞典语和芬兰语历史报纸文本），MDPO优于监督微调与单负样本DPO，尤其在NIL提及、语义歧义、OCR噪声和历史困难名称方面提升明显。进一步分析表明候选检索仍是端到端实体链接的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 将所有实例内负候选纳入偏好优化是一种简单而有效的改进策略，可显著提升基于LLM的历史实体链接性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently shown promise for historical entity linking, but preference optimization for this task is often formulated with only one negative candidate per training instance. This discards information from the remaining candidates retrieved for the same mention. We introduce multi-negative direct preference optimisation (MDPO), a reference-based pairwise objective that compares the correct entity with all valid rejected candidates associated with each mention. MDPO preserves the Bradley-Terry formulation of DPO while exploiting the complete candidate set through masked, length-normalised sequence scores. We evaluate MDPO on hipe-2020 and newseye, covering French, German, English, Swedish, and Finnish historical newspaper text. Experiments show that MDPO improves over supervised fine-tuning and single-negative DPO, with particularly strong gains for NIL mentions, semantic ambiguity, OCR noise, and historically difficult names. Further analyses disentangle candidate-generation and selection errors, showing that candidate retrieval remains a key bottleneck for end-to-end entity linking. These results demonstrate that incorporating all within-instance negative candidates is a simple and effective improvement for LLM-based historical entity linking.

</details>


### [188] [An LLM-Associated Register Shift in Korean Journal Abstracts: A Morphology-Aware Excess-Vocabulary Study, 2018-2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07447)
*Aron Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将"超额词汇"（excess vocabulary）这一衡量2022年后学术英语变化的指标适配到韩语，使用形态学单元分析了398,296篇KCI摘要（2018年至2026年8月），并以47,165篇越南语摘要作为对照。研究发现韩语摘要中LLM加工的痕迹2023年未见，2024年底出现并一路上升至2026年中期趋平；特定词汇如"sisahada"（暗示）的异常高出现频率可作为信号，而普通动词如"araboda"（调查）则显著下降。基于单词语统计的LLM处理摘要条件下限估计为2024-2026年分别3.5%、10.5%和16.1%，基于分半集合的估计为7.8%、20.6%和33.0%。多种稳健性检验（主题控制、翻译路线检验、人工生成对照摘要等）均不能解释该现象，表明韩语学术写作中LLM使用正在显著增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的"超额词汇"方法已被用于量化2022年后学术英语中LLM的使用变化，但尚不清楚这一现象在非英语学术写作中是否同样存在。作者希望将这一方法适配到韩语学术界，考察韩语学术摘要是否也出现了由LLM生成或加工文本的上升趋势，并探讨其时间进程、规模及可能的替代解释。

Method: 作者将超额词汇定义为某词在此前趋势线上方的频率超额部分，并首次将这一方法用于韩语，采用形态学单位处理398,296篇KCI摘要（2018至2026年8月），以47,165篇越南语摘要作为跨语言对照。设置了安慰剂下限（placebo floors）检验统计量的虚假阳性率。通过单词语统计与分半重组集合统计两种方法估计LLM处理摘要的下限比例；引入Holzwarth等人的估计器作为交叉验证；并通过主题变量控制、剔除语言模型标注者认定的风格词、与基线期最近配对、翻译路线检验（检查韩语翻译痕迹是否与标记词反向变化）、以及来自三个供应商的人工生成对照摘要等多重稳健性分析排除替代解释。

Result: 单词语统计的安慰剂地板为0.1-2.2点，分半集合统计至多2.9点。韩语摘要中2023年无异常信号，2024年底开始出现，2025年上升并在2026年中趋平。"sisahada"（暗示）出现在21.4%的2026年摘要中，而预期仅5.3%；"araboda"（调查）等普通动词降至趋势水平的四分之一。在既定假设下，2024-2026年LLM处理摘要的单词语条件下限分别为3.5%、10.5%和16.1%，分半集合下限为7.8%、20.6%和33.0%；Holzwarth等人的估计器给出2025-2026年为41.9%和72.1%。主题控制不能移除效应：限定为三位语言模型标注者一致认定的风格词仍保留33.0个点中的14.7个点，与基期最近配对后保留34.1。翻译路线无法解释：韩语翻译的表面标记随标记词上升而下降。英文摘要中该现象早一年出现；英文侧无异常时韩语移位仍以英文侧有异常时的30-66%速率持续。三个提供商的对照摘要复现了上升词，词目周转与模型世代一致，但隐含流行率依赖于场景设定。

Conclusion: 韩语学术写作中存在与英语学术界类似的LLM加工摘要上升趋势，但其时间进程滞后于英语约一年。该现象在2024年底开启，到2026年中期增速趋平，且不能被翻译、主题偏差或简短写作风格等替代因素解释。多种估计方法给出的LLM比例范围宽（约16%-72%取决于方法、年份和假设），提示虽然证据确凿支持LLM使用增长，但精确流行率的点估计对方法选择和场景假设高度敏感。

Abstract: Excess vocabulary, a word's frequency above its pre-2023 trend, is how the change in scholarly English after 2022 has been measured. We adapt it to Korean with morphological units on 398,296 KCI abstracts (2018-August 2026), with 47,165 Vietnamese abstracts for comparison. Placebo floors are 0.1-2.2 points for the single-word statistic and at most 2.9 for the re-selected split-half set statistic. Korean abstracts show nothing in 2023, onset in late 2024, a rise through 2025 flattening in mid-2026: sisahada "suggest" appears in 21.4% of 2026 abstracts against 5.3% expected; plain verbs like araboda "look into" fall to a quarter of trend. Under stated assumptions the single-word conditional lower bound on LLM-processed abstracts is 3.5%, 10.5% and 16.1% for 2024-2026 and a split-half set bound 7.8%, 20.6% and 33.0%. Holzwarth et al.'s estimator under the same discipline gives 41.9% and 72.1% for 2025-2026. Subject-matter controls reduce but do not remove it: restricting the set to lemmas three language-model annotators all call style leaves 14.7 of the 33.0 points, and pairing each 2026 abstract with its journal's closest base-period abstract leaves 34.1. Tested translation routes do not explain it: the surface marks of translated Korean fall as the markers rise. In the same articles' English abstracts the excess appears a year earlier; where the English side carries none, the Korean shift persists at 30 to 66% of the rate where it does. Control abstracts from three providers reproduce the rising words, with marker turnover consistent with model generations; implied prevalences are scenario-dependent.

</details>


### [189] [FramingQA: Does the Question Shape the Answer? Measuring the Compositional Framing Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07448)
*Hazel H. Kim,Andrew M. Bean,Guilherme Affonso Ferreira de Camargo,Shanyu Chauhan,Felix Drinkall,Jade Kosché,Chenyang Ma,Glory Nwaugbala,Nabeel Seedat,Bradley Max Segal,Samuel Recht,Hinrich Schütze,Philip H. S. Torr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FramingQA基准，用于衡量大语言模型对问题框架（framing）偏见的敏感性。该基准覆盖法律、医学、金融和机器人模拟四个高风险领域，通过三个嵌套层级（root、propositional、global）注入框架偏见。评估了四个模型家族共九个开源模型（3.8B-70B），发现即便模型在单个变体上准确率高，也无法保证在不同措辞的问题下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对带有隐含立场的细微重新措辞会改变回答，导致用户的建议被问题措辞方式而非事实本身所污染。在高风险领域（法律、医疗、金融）这种后果代价高昂。现实场景中专家和非专家用户都经常提出包含不完整或误导性假设的问题，因此模型极易受框架偏见影响，需要系统性衡量这一问题。

Method: 构建FramingQA基准，设计三个嵌套层级的框架偏见注入方式：(1) root层——带有框架偏见的问题措辞本身；(2) propositional层——在中性问题前注入框架偏见的前提；(3) global层——前提与偏见问题同时出现。在固定事实信息不变的情况下评估模型对四种领域、九个开源模型（3.8B-70B，覆盖四个模型家族）的表现。

Result: 实验发现，模型在不同变体上的强准确率并不能保证在不同措辞的问题下（固定事实信息不变时）保持鲁棒性，说明框架偏见对LLM回答存在系统性影响。

Conclusion: 现有的LLM在高风险领域中对问题框架存在显著敏感性，单一评测维度的强性能不足以证明模型的可靠性；需要专门的基准来衡量和缓解这一框架偏见问题。

Abstract: We introduce FramingQA, a benchmark that measures the model sensitivity to question framing across law, medicine, finance, and robotic simulations. Large language models (LLMs) often change their responses to subtle rephrasings that align with an implied stance by users. This can leave users with advice tainted by how they happened to phrase a question rather than by the underlying facts, and the consequences are highly costly in high-stakes domains. Because in the realistic scenarios, both expert practitioners and non-expert users frequently ask LLMs questions containing incomplete or misleading assumptions, models are highly susceptible to those framings. To test this, we inject the framing bias across three nested levels: a framing-biased question phrasing (root), an injected framing-biased premise prepended to a neutral question (propositional), and a premise paired with a framing-biased question (global). Evaluating nine open models (3.8B-70B) across four families, we find that strong per-variant accuracy does not guarantee the robustness across differently phrased questions under the fixed factual information.

</details>


### [190] [MEMO: Multimodal Evidence Memory Organization for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07471)
*Xian Gao,Jinpeng Wang,Jiacheng Ruan,Guangyu Cao,Ting Liu,Yuzhuo Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MEMO，一种多模态证据记忆组织方法，通过训练证据提取器、查询条件下的记忆管理器和确定性记忆构建模块，自动为LLM智能体将相关记忆块分配到文本、视觉或双通道载体并匹配布局，解决长时运行智能体中有限上下文预算与持续积累的交互轨迹之间的张力。在四个基准上验证了更高效、更有效的记忆组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的LLM智能体依赖外部记忆在单一上下文窗口之外存储和复用信息，但交互轨迹的持续积累与有限的上下文容量之间存在根本矛盾。现有文本记忆保真度高但线性表示导致不同重要性内容以近均匀成本竞争有限上下文；视觉记忆利用二维布局突出结构但渲染和压缩可能丢失细节。因此需要新的方法来在给定预算下选择必要证据并以适当模态组织记忆。

Method: MEMO包含三个核心组件：(1) 训练的证据提取器，从记忆块中选择相关记录并形成带来源信息和呈现需求的证据单元；(2) 查询条件下的记忆管理器，将每个证据单元分配到文本、视觉或双通道载体并选择与证据结构匹配的布局，训练时使用离线阅读器反馈来对齐保留和呈现决策；(3) 确定性记忆构建模块，生成文本包和视觉页面。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA、LoCoMo和ALFWorld四个基准上使用多种阅读器后端的评估表明，MEMO能以更少的记忆token更高效地呈现记忆，提升下游任务性能，并在受限预算下构建更有效的工作记忆。

Conclusion: MEMO通过多模态证据记忆组织有效解决了LLM智能体记忆中保留与呈现的联合优化问题，验证了根据证据结构和查询需求动态分配文本/视觉载体并以紧凑布局呈现是一种优于单一模态的记忆读出方案。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents rely on external memory to store and reuse information beyond a single context window, yet there is a fundamental tension between the continuous accumulation of interaction trajectories and the limited context capacity. The key challenge in agent memory is therefore not only to retrieve relevant records, but also to select necessary evidence under a given budget and organize it in an appropriate modality. Existing memory readout methods mainly use textual or visual forms. Text preserves high fidelity, but its linear token representation makes contents with different importance compete for the limited context at nearly uniform unit cost. Visual readout renders text into document-like images, which can use two-dimensional layouts to expose structure and emphasize key information, but it may lose fine-grained details during rendering and compression. To address this issue, we propose MEMO, a multimodal evidence memory organization method for LLM agents. MEMO first uses a trained evidence extractor to select relevant memory blocks and form evidence units with source information and presentation requirements. A trained query-conditioned memory manager assigns each unit to a textual, visual, or dual-channel carrier and selects a layout that matches the evidence structure. A deterministic memory construction module then generates the textual package and visual pages. The memory manager is trained with feedback from an offline reader that measures the utility of the guided memory plan, so that retention and presentation decisions align with downstream usage. We evaluate MEMO on four benchmarks, HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, LoCoMo, and ALFWorld, with multiple reader backends. The results show that MEMO presents memory more efficiently with fewer memory tokens, improves downstream task performance, and builds more effective working memory under constrained budgets.

</details>


### [191] [Where Should Language Sit in a Multimodal Model? Lessons from What Language Does to Human Perception and Cognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07474)
*Peng Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过对照人类大脑中语言的作用机制，审视语言模型和多模态模型中语言的位置。作者将语言视为运行在共享码本上的压缩器，在人类中这种压缩可测量、学习码本会重组感官、思想在失去语言后仍能存活。通过对六个视觉-语言模型和两个机器人策略进行线索冲突实验，发现模型按可靠性加权幸存线索（达到理想观察者斜率的11-82%），许多答案直接复制文本；有的策略丢弃无附加信息的线索，有的保留冲突时失败的权重，视觉上识别任务的线索因语言通路已拟合数据而从未被学习。作者总结七条启示：语言应位于模型边界和共享码本中，如大脑一般，而非作为内部表示，内部化码本的代价是可审计性的丧失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型用token计算：语言既是输入、输出，也日益成为内部表示。语言是否应占据所有这些位置取决于语言对使用它的系统所起的作用。唯一拥有百年数据解答该问题的系统是人类。作者因此回顾语言对人类知觉、大脑和思维的作用，并以此证据审视多模态模型和语言模型。

Method: 本文先做综述性论证，将语言视为"运行在共享码本上的压缩器"（一个词是索引，内容在接收端，社群维护码本）。随后设计了线索冲突（cue-conflict）实验，在六个视觉-语言模型和两个机器人策略上测试两种线索不一致时模型如何加权；对比人类证据中语言对感知的重组作用及失语状态下思维的存活。

Result: 模型按线索可靠性的顺序加权幸存线索，斜率达到理想观察者的11-82%；许多答案直接复制文本。一个策略家族丢弃不增加信息的线索，另一个保留在冲突时失效的权重；在所有训练帧中都能识别任务的视觉线索从未被学到，因为语言通路已充分拟合数据。语言模型是人类语言网络最好的当前模型，且已进入人类言语社群，改变词频、对齐缩小概念多样性。

Conclusion: 语言应位于模型的边界和共享码本中（如同大脑），不应成为模型的内部表示。让码本留在内部将付出可审计性（auditability）的代价。全文末尾给出针对token驱动系统的七条启示。

Abstract: Language models compute over tokens: language is their input, their output, and increasingly their internal representation. Whether language should keep all of these positions depends on what language does to the system that uses it. The one system with a century of data on that question is the human. We review what language does to human perception, the brain, and thought, and read the same evidence against multimodal models and language models. Throughout, we treat language as a compressor that runs on a shared codebook: a word is an index, the content is in the receiver, and a community maintains the codebook. In humans the compression is measurable, learning the codebook reorganizes the senses, and thought survives the loss of language. We then measure the rule that models apply when two cues disagree, with cue-conflict experiments on six vision-language models and two robot policies. Surviving cues are weighted in the order their reliabilities prescribe, at 11 to 82\% of the ideal observer's slope, and many answers copy the text. One policy family drops a cue that adds no information beyond the others rather than down-weighting it, another keeps it at a weight that fails when the cues conflict, and a visual cue that identifies the task in every training frame is never learned, because the language pathway already fits the data. Language models are the best current models of the human language network, and they have entered the human speech community, shifting word frequencies while alignment narrows their conceptual diversity. We close with seven implications for token-based systems. Language belongs at a model's boundary and in the shared codebook, as in the brain, not as its internal representation; the price of leaving the codebook inside is auditability.

</details>


### [192] [Qwen-Audio-3.0-ASR Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07549)
*Chuanmeng Bian,Daren Chen,Peixin Chen,Zhigao Chen,Zhiyun Fan,Zhifu Gao,Bo Gong,Qing Gu,Jiajun He,Yawei Hu,Yunjie Ji,Jingbei Li,Xiangang Li,Xu Li,Zengxi Li,Zheng Li,Chengdong Liang,Baiji Liu,Ying Liu,Bin Ma,Yiping Peng,Yuezhang Peng,Zhendong Peng,Yu Pu,Yang Shi,Xin Shu,Jian Tang,Biao Tian,Peiyao Wang,Tianzi Wang,Wen Wang,Wupeng Wang,Cheng Wen,Yuzhong Wu,Zijian Xia,Yunchong Xiao,Nan Yang,Jianwei Yu,Jixing Yu,Binbin Zhang,Lei Zhang,Sitong Zhao,Guangdong Zhou,Yuan Zhou,Jianheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Qwen-Audio-3.0-ASR，一个基于MoE和LLM的语音识别系统，支持30种语言和16种中文方言，并提供领域实体识别、热词定制、转写优化和长音频建模等面向生产环境的能力，还包含面向低延迟场景的流式变体，在多项评测中达到SOTA或具有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术基准性能与实际工业应用之间仍存在显著差距，尤其是在区域方言、动态实体与热词、长上下文信息以及非流利自发言语等生产性挑战上，现有ASR系统难以兼顾，需要构建一个统一的、指令跟随式的生产级ASR系统来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 基于Qwen骨干网络构建Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）LLM-based ASR系统，在数千万小时的大规模语音数据上训练。系统采用统一指令跟随框架，支持30种语言和16种中文方言（覆盖八大方言区），并提供生产导向能力：工业领域实体识别、层级化热词定制、单遍转写润色和长音频上下文建模。此外，开发了专用的流式变体用于低延迟场景。

Result: 在中文、英文、多语言及真实工业测试集上的广泛评测中，系统达到SOTA或极具竞争力的识别性能，相较包括GPT-4o Transcribe和Gemini 3.1 Pro在内的领先商业及专有系统表现优越。

Conclusion: Qwen-Audio-3.0-ASR证明了将数据扩展、模型扩展与LLM深度融合三种范式整合到MoE架构中，能够有效弥合学术基准与生产应用之间的差距，在方言处理、热词定制、长上下文及自发语音等复杂场景下提供生产级ASR能力，流式变体也进一步拓展了其低延迟应用场景。

Abstract: In recent years, automatic speech recognition (ASR) has witnessed transformative advancements driven by three complementary paradigms: data scaling, model scaling, and deep integration with large language models (LLMs). However, bridging the gap between academic benchmark performance and real-world production utility remains a persistent challenge, particularly in handling diverse regional dialects, dynamic entities and hotwords, long-range contextual information, and disfluent spontaneous speech. In this report, we present Qwen-Audio-3.0-ASR, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLM-based ASR system designed to address these production demands through a unified, instruction-following framework. The model is built upon the Qwen backbone, and is trained on tens of millions of hours of large-scale speech data. Qwen-Audio-3.0-ASR supports transcription across 30 languages and 16 Chinese dialectal varieties spanning eight major dialect regions. Beyond multilingual and dialectal recognition, the model provides production-oriented capabilities including industry-domain entity recognition, hierarchical hotword customization, native single-pass transcription polishing, and long-audio contextual modeling. We further develop a dedicated streaming variant, Qwen-Audio-3.0-ASR-Streaming, for latency-sensitive applications. Extensive evaluations on Chinese, English, multilingual, and real-world industrial test sets demonstrate state-of-the-art or highly competitive recognition performance across a broad range of evaluation conditions, with strong performance relative to leading commercial and proprietary systems including GPT-4o Transcribe and Gemini 3.1 Pro.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [ECHO: Dyadic 3D Facial Motion Generation with Asymmetric Deterministic Articulation and Stochastic Reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05506)
*Zhuoqiang Cai,Yujie Sun,Chaoyue Niu,Hongyun Yu,Zhiwen Chen,Chengfei Lv,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: ECHO是一种用于双人3D面部运动生成的框架，在严格双流音频-only设定下将问题建模为非对称任务（确定性说话的锚点+随机听者的残差），通过运动记忆作为仅训练期正则化和语义分组缩放控制残差注入，在单次生成过程中平衡说话端的发音保真度与听者端的真实感和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双人对话情境中，3D面部运动生成是一个非对称问题：说话者的面部运动（尤其是发音相关部分）受语音强约束，而听者的反应则是一对多的随机交互行为。现有方法通常对所有运动分量统一施加随机性或确定性建模，无法同时兼顾说话端的发音保真度和听者端的真实感与多样性。

Method: ECHO将运动分解为两部分：(1) 确定性锚点——捕获与语音相关的稳定结构；(2) 随机残差——建模剩余的一对多交互动态。骨架之上引入Motion Memory作为仅训练期的正则化器，在短暂的后期微调阶段为弱条件听者窗口提供局部先验；语义组缩放（semantic-group scaling）则分别控制残差在表情、下颌和颈部区域的注入强度。

Result: 统一评估、逐状态评估和消融实验表明：将稳定与不确定运动组件解耦，优于对各分量均匀施加随机性的方法。对话3D面部运动生成从显式分解可预测与不可预测成分中获益。

Conclusion: ECHO为在严格音频-only条件下构建可部署的对话式数字人提供了实用的问题形式化与技术基础，能在单一生成过程中兼顾说话端的发音表达和听者端的反应真实性与多样性。

Abstract: We propose ECHO for dyadic 3D facial motion generation under a strict dual-stream audio-only setting, formulating the problem as an asymmetric task involving speech-constrained articulation and one-to-many listener reactions. To address this asymmetry, ECHO decomposes motion into a deterministic anchor that captures stable speech-correlated structure and a stochastic residual that models the remaining one-to-many interaction dynamics. On top of this backbone, Motion Memory acts as a training-only regularizer during brief late-stage fine-tuning to provide local priors for weakly conditioned listening windows, while semantic-group scaling controls residual injection across expression, jaw, and neck. This design balances speaking-side articulatory fidelity with listening-side realism and diversity in a single generation process. Results from unified, state-wise, and ablation evaluations show that conversational 3D motion benefits from decomposing stable and uncertain components rather than applying stochasticity uniformly. ECHO provides a practical formulation and technical basis for deployable conversational digital humans under strict audio-only conditions.

</details>


### [194] [RBF Your SDF: Radial Basis Function Interpolation of Signed Distance Fields with Implied Tangent Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06209)
*Yong Cheng,Yotam Gingold*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将切球观测与径向基函数插值相结合的SDF隐式几何重建方法，通过识别极约束切点配置来保留尖锐特征（角点），并采用partition-of-unity分解扩展到大规模网格，在Chamfer和Hausdorff精度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重建方法（行进立方体、对偶等值面）忽略了远离曲面的样本信息。近期工作利用SDF的切球结构，但基于表面重构的零级集提取只考虑表面上的点与法线，仍丢弃其他样本，且在尖锐特征处系统性圆角化。作者希望整合全部样本并保留角点。

Method: 将SDF样本的切球观测与所有数据（隐含表面点及原始数据）的径向基函数插值相结合。通过检测切线点极度受限的球体（几何上在尖锐表面特征处必然出现的配置）来识别并保留表面角点。采用partition-of-unity分解以支持大规模网格分辨率的高效扩展。

Result: 在每种测试分辨率下，重建结果在Chamfer距离和Hausdorff距离精度上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 作者的方法通过整合切球信息与全量RBF插值，并利用受限切点检测角点，在保留尖锐特征的同时获得更高的几何精度，且可扩展到大规模网格。

Abstract: Signed distance fields (SDFs) are a popular implicit representation of geometry. Converting a discrete set of SDF samples into an explicit surface is a fundamental problem in geometry processing. Traditional reconstruction methods such as marching cubes and dual contouring ignore the geometric information carried by samples far from the surface. Recently, Sellán et al.[2023] and several follow-up works leveraged the tangent-sphere structure of SDFs; every sample implies a point on a sphere tangent to the surface. However, these approaches extract the zero-level set via surface reconstruction, which considers only points and normals on the surface and ignores the remaining samples. We propose an approach that marries the tangent-sphere observation with radial basis function interpolation of all data, the implied surface points and the original data. By detecting spheres with extremely constrained tangent points, a configuration geometrically forced at sharp surface features, we identify and preserve surface corners that surface reconstruction-based methods systematically round. A partition-of-unity decomposition allows our method to scale efficiently to large grid resolutions. Our reconstructions improve both Chamfer and Hausdorff accuracy at every tested resolution.

</details>


### [195] [Skinned Motion Retargeting via Artifact-driven Kinematic Prior Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06517)
*Seokhyeon Hong,Chaelin Kim,Inseo Jang,Soojin Choi,Junyong Noh*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个几何感知的运动重定向框架，通过显式地将姿态角色几何中的自穿透伪影与运动细化关联起来，同时保持骨架无关的神经重定向灵活性，在固定和任意骨架结构上均提升了运动精度并减少了几何伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经运动重定向方法虽然在不同骨架间具有灵活性，但难以解决目标侧几何伪影（如自穿透）。已有几何感知方法依赖固定骨架模板或隐式几何条件预测，要求单个网络同时处理目标几何变形、检测伪影并仅从目标几何进行修正，限制了其在多样骨架和体型上的泛化能力。

Method: 提出一个统一框架：(1) 使用基于transformer的重定向自编码器学习跨不同骨架共享的运动嵌入，实现任意源-目标骨架对的运动迁移；(2) 引入伪影驱动的细化模块，通过运动到顶点Jacobian将目标网格上的自穿透观察转换为修正提示；(3) 基于静态姿态网格和蒙皮权重提取的关节对齐几何特征来条件化运动解码，整合显式目标侧伪影推理与灵活几何感知解码。

Result: 在固定和任意骨架结构设置下的实验表明，该方法提高了运动学重定向精度并减少了几何伪影，能够在已见和未见的目标角色上产生合理的运动。

Conclusion: 通过显式连接角色几何中的伪影与运动细化，同时保留骨架无关重定向的灵活性，该框架有效克服了既有方法在泛化性和几何质量之间的权衡，在多样化角色上实现更准确且无伪影的运动重定向。

Abstract: Motion retargeting aims to transfer a source motion to target characters with different skeletal structures, proportions, and body shapes. Although recent neural retargeting methods have improved flexibility across diverse skeletons, target-side geometric artifacts such as self-penetration remain difficult to resolve. Specifically, existing geometry-aware approaches often rely on fixed skeleton templates or implicit geometry-conditioned prediction, requiring a single network to account for target geometry deformation, detect target-side artifacts, and predict the corresponding correction from target geometry alone, which limits their ability to generalize across diverse skeleton structures and body shapes. In this paper, we present a geometry-aware motion retargeting framework that explicitly connects artifacts observed in the posed character geometry to motion refinement while preserving the flexibility of skeleton-agnostic neural retargeting. Our method first learns a motion embedding shared across different skeletons using a transformer-based retargeting autoencoder that transfers motion across arbitrary source--target skeleton pairs. Building on this kinematic motion prior, we introduce an artifact-driven refinement module that observes self-penetration on the posed target mesh and converts it into a corrective cue through a motion-to-vertex Jacobian. We further condition motion decoding on target geometry using skinning weight-based joint-aligned geometry features derived from the rest pose mesh. This design combines explicit target-side artifact reasoning with flexible geometry-aware decoding in a unified framework. Experiments on both fixed and arbitrary skeleton structure settings show that our method improves kinematic retargeting accuracy and reduces geometric artifacts, producing plausible motions across seen and unseen target characters.

</details>


### [196] [CT2Yarn: Yarn-Level Reconstruction of Crochet from Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06950)
*Chang Luo,Nobuyuki Umetani*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出CT2Yarn，一个结合人工介入的框架，用于从真实钩针编织物的微型计算机断层扫描（micro-CT）中恢复单一的连续纱线路径。该方法通过Gabor滤波估计局部纤维方向，用各向异性均值漂移聚合得到纱线级方向估计，再结合自动拓扑骨架化、片段链接与环路检测，辅以草图式用户界面交互补全，最终恢复完整纱线路径，支持物理模拟、纱线级渲染和针法模式提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钩针编织物由单根纱线通过互锁环路形成复杂三维形状，从外部观测恢复纱线路径因严重的自遮挡而极具挑战。虽然micro-CT能够揭示物体内部完整结构，但由于纱线具有层级结构（纱线由多股加捻纱线组成，每股又由加捻纤维构成），体扫描中的局部纤维方向与整体纱线方向并不一致，导致体积扫描本身无法显式编码纱线的走向。因此需要专门的方法从纤维级方向恢复纱线级路径。

Method: CT2Yarn的流程为：1）使用Gabor滤波从micro-CT体数据中估计局部纤维方向，并将其转换为带方向的点云；2）提出各向异性均值漂移（anisotropic mean-shift）过程，在纱线半径邻域内聚合局部纤维方向，得到纱线级的方向估计；3）结合自动拓扑骨架化策略提取纱线路径片段；4）通过片段链接、结点清理和环路检测将这些骨架树合并为少量长曲线；5）在自动重建存在歧义之处，提供基于草图（sketch-based）的用户界面让用户交互式地完成单一连续纱线路径的恢复。

Result: 该方法成功从真实钩针编织物的micro-CT扫描中恢复了单一的连续纱线路径，解决了层级结构导致的纤维方向与纱线方向不一致问题。恢复的纱线路径可支持物理仿真、纱线级渲染和针迹模式提取等下游应用。

Conclusion: CT2Yarn通过结合自动算法（Gabor滤波、各向异性均值漂移、骨架化、环路检测）与人工交互（草图式用户界面），实现了从micro-CT扫描中恢复钩针编织物完整连续纱线路径的目标，为基于纱线层级结构的编织物分析提供了可行的计算框架。

Abstract: We introduce CT2Yarn, a human-in-the-loop framework for recovering a single continuous yarn path from micro-computed tomography (micro-CT) scans of real crochet objects. Crochet is a craft that creates complex three-dimensional shapes by interlocking loops formed from a single yarn. Recovering the underlying yarn path from external observations is challenging because of severe self-occlusion. While micro-CT reveals the full internal structure of a crochet object, the volumetric scan alone does not explicitly encode how the yarn traverses the object. This challenge stems from the hierarchical structure of yarn: a yarn consists of multiple twisted plies, and each ply itself consists of twisted fibers. Consequently, local fiber orientations observed in micro-CT scans are not aligned with the overall yarn direction. To recover yarn-level orientations from ply-level fiber orientations, we first estimate local fiber directions using Gabor filtering and convert the volume into an oriented point cloud. We then introduce an anisotropic mean-shift procedure that aggregates local fiber orientations within a neighborhood of the yarn radius into yarn-level orientation estimates. Combined with an automatic topology skeletonization strategy, our method extracts yarn-path fragments. Subsequent fragment linking, junction cleaning, and loop detection merge these trees into a small number of long curves. Where the automatic reconstruction remains ambiguous, a sketch-based user interface enables users to interactively complete the single continuous yarn path. The recovered yarn path enables downstream applications including physically based simulation, ply-level rendering, and stitch-pattern extraction.

</details>


### [197] [Neural Centroidal Voronoi Tessellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08497)
*Jiacheng Xu,Bo Pang,Rui Xu,Xiaocheng Zhang,Yang Liu,Fei Zhu,Guoping Wang,Peng-Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出Neural CVT，一种基于学习的曲面质心Voronoi剖分（CVT）求解器。它通过图形神经网络编码多尺度几何描述符，并用轻量级循环优化器迭代细化种子点位置，将传统CVT优化加速1-2个数量级，同时保持几何保真度。模型采用自监督训练，可在未见几何、初始化策略和种子密度上泛化，生成各向同性且保持特征的重网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统曲面CVT求解代价高昂，每一步优化都需要反复构建受限Voronoi图（RVD）并在其曲面单元上进行积分。这种昂贵的几何计算限制了CVT作为高质量曲面采样和各向同性重网格基元的实用效率，亟需更高效的求解方案。

Method: 给定输入曲面，先采样稠密点云，用图神经网络编码器提取多尺度几何描述符；随后一个轻量级循环优化器通过少量迭代细化种子位置，通过聚合插值的曲面特征和优化历史来预测每个种子的位移。整个框架使用自监督CVT目标（促进均匀分布和特征对齐）进行训练。

Result: 在多种有机和CAD类形状基准上，Neural CVT相比传统优化方法提速1-2个数量级，同时保持几何保真度。模型能泛化到未见几何、不同初始化策略和种子密度，生成与最先进离线优化方法相当的各向同性且保持特征的重网格。

Conclusion: Neural CVT证明学习型方法可以替代CVT求解中昂贵的RVD构建与曲面积分步骤，在不牺牲几何质量的前提下实现数量级的性能提升，并且具有良好的泛化能力。代码与训练模型将公开。

Abstract: Centroidal Voronoi tessellation (CVT) is a fundamental primitive for high-quality surface sampling and isotropic remeshing in computer graphics. However, computing surface CVTs with classical solvers remains expensive: each optimization step repeatedly constructs restricted Voronoi diagrams (RVDs) and integrates quantities over their surface cells. We introduce Neural CVT, a learning-based surface-CVT solver that replaces these costly geometric computations with a recurrent neural optimizer, accelerating CVT optimization by one to two orders of magnitude in our benchmarks while preserving geometric fidelity. Given an input surface, we sample a dense point cloud and extract multi-scale geometric descriptors with a graph neural encoder. A lightweight recurrent optimizer then refines seed positions over a small number of iterations, aggregating interpolated surface features and optimization history to predict per-seed displacements. The framework is trained self-supervised using CVT objectives that promote uniform distributions and, when desired, feature alignment. Across diverse organic and CAD-like shapes, Neural CVT generalizes to unseen geometry, initialization strategies, and seed densities, producing isotropic, feature-preserving remeshes comparable to state-of-the-art offline optimization methods at a fraction of the computational cost. Code and trained models will be released.

</details>


### [198] [Inverse Digital Marbling: Recovering Gesture Programs with a Replay Adjoint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08722)
*Tianqi Liu,Yushan Han,Hang Liu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种针对数字大理石纹（paper marbling）沉积模型的程序恢复方法：给定目标图像，通过优化有序的胶囊插入程序（2000个手势、1024²分辨率）对其进行近似回放，恢复了可编辑的可执行程序，并且相比检查点自动微分方案节省8.7倍显存，在单工作站GPU上约4分钟完成拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大理石纹图案中颜料的沉积会位移已有图案，使得每个手势的外观与后续动作耦合，这说明图案是由有序手势程序生成的。研究者希望从目标图像逆向恢复出可执行的手势编辑程序，从而实现程序级的可编辑性（如插入、重着色、平移等编辑），而不仅仅是得到与图像相似的像素结果。
