On climate discourse, the correction improves the net steering effect from +5.9% to +14.0%, validated over 100,000 pairwise judgments.

</details>


### [231] [Evidence-Aligned Local Composition of Discrete Experts for Sequence Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05801)
*Mohammad Panahazari,Usman A. Khan,Shuchin Aeron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"证据对齐的局部组合"（evidence-aligned local composition）方法，在测试时无区域标签或训练路由器的条件下，通过从损坏观测的边际证据推断每个位置的专家软权重，实现对多领域混合文档的修复与专家选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档可视为不同领域文本（如README中的自然语言、代码、配置）的序列组合。当文档被损坏且仅有固定的领域专家可用时，恢复文档需要同时判断缺失内容以及每个位置应信任哪个专家。然而在测试时既没有区域标签也没有训练好的路由器，传统单一全局权重方法无法处理位置级混合组成。

Method: 提出证据对齐的局部组合方法：在给定损坏模型下，从损坏观测的边际证据中推断每个位置上的软权重（对各领域专家的位置级加权分配）。具体从专家自身的去噪损失估计证据，并在位置间平滑权重。权重是软性的，当真实组成是混合时能恢复混合物，当单一专家足够时则集中于该专家。

Result: 在类别模拟器、字节级专家以及基于1.3B离散流匹配模型微调的专家上验证：在自然混合的科学文档上达到0.85的字段准确率，在跨域词法分离的构造混合上达到0.98。当专家真正不同时，恢复效果优于单一全局权重；当专家趋于收敛时退化为全局权重，与专家分离度的度量保持一致。

Conclusion: 该方法无需区域标签或训练路由器，即可在测试时自适应地对文档各位置进行专家加权，灵活应对混合组成，并随专家差异性自适应调整，在修复效果上优于或等价于单一全局权重基线。

Abstract: A document modeled as a discrete sequence of tokens can be thought of as being generated from a composition of texts from different domains; a README file, for example, moves between prose, code, and configuration. When such a document is corrupted and only frozen domain experts are available, restoring it requires deciding both what is missing and which expert to trust at each position, at test time and without region labels or a trained router. We introduce evidence-aligned local composition, which infers a soft, position-wise weighting over the experts from the marginal evidence of the corrupted observation under a given corruption model, estimating the evidence from the experts' own denoising losses and smoothing the weights across positions. Because the weighting is soft, it recovers a mixture when the true composition is mixed and concentrates on one expert when that suffices. Across a categorical simulator, byte-level experts, and experts fine-tuned from a $1.3$B discrete flow-matching model, the inferred weights track the true regions at $0.85$ field accuracy on naturally mixed scientific documents, and at $0.98$ on constructed mixtures whose regions are lexically disjoint. Restoration improves over a single global weight when the experts are genuinely distinct and reduces to it when they converge, tracking a measure of expert separation.

</details>


### [232] [Exposing Weaknesses in Emotion Recognition in Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05806)
*Amir Ben Khalifa,Fanny Bezancon,Amine Trabelsi,Bessam Abdulrazak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑ERC任务中"每个话语可分配单一明确情感标签"的核心假设，通过零样本LLM实验发现模型错误集中在否定、感叹和感叹词等话语类型中，受控再标注研究显示人类标注者仅在35%的案例中达成强一致性，表明许多看似模型错误的实为标注歧义，因此提出LLM-as-Judge的独立情感评估框架替代单标签评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ERC领域普遍依赖任务特定微调并假设每个话语只有一个无歧义的情感标签，但这一核心假设从未被系统检验。研究旨在探究单标签假设是否成立，以及模型报告的错误中多大比例实为标注歧义所致。

Method: 在零样本设置下使用LLM进行ERC评测，结合前序对话轮次作为上下文；对错误进行系统性分析，按话语语言特征（否定、感叹、感叹词）归类；进行四名人类标注者的受控再标注研究以测量互标注一致性；提出LLM-as-Judge框架，对每种情感独立评估其在对话语境中的合理性而非强制单标签输出。

Result: 聚合指标掩盖了系统性失败；错误集中于含否定、感叹和感叹词的话语，且该模式在所有评估模型中一致出现，指向基准局限而非模型缺陷。再标注研究中人类强一致性仅出现在35%的案例中，且中性话语占据高一致性多数，许多情感类别落入低一致区域。

Conclusion: 许多表面上的模型错误反映的是真实标注歧义而非情感理解能力不足，标准单标签评估不足以可靠衡量ERC模型；所提出的LLM-as-Judge框架通过逐情感独立判断其上下文合理性，为ERC评测提供更可靠的替代方案。

Abstract: Emotion Recognition in Conversations (ERC) aims to identify speakers' emotions in multi-turn dialogue. Accurate emotion recognition can support a wide range of applications, including empathetic conversational agents, mental health support, and educational technologies. While many recent approaches rely on task-specific fine-tuning, such models may exploit dataset-specific cues. A central yet rarely questioned assumption in ERC is that each utterance can be assigned a single unambiguous emotion label. To investigate this assumption, we study ERC using Large Language Models (LLMs) in a zero-shot setting while incorporating preceding conversational turns as context. We show that aggregate metrics mask systematic failures. Errors concentrate around utterances containing negations, exclamations, and interjections. This pattern is consistent across all evaluated models, suggesting limitations in the benchmarks rather than model-specific weaknesses. A controlled re-annotation study involving four human annotators supports this finding: strong agreement is observed in only 35 percent of cases, with neutral utterances dominating high-agreement instances, while many emotional categories fall into low-agreement regimes. These findings suggest that many apparent model errors reflect genuine annotation ambiguity rather than poor emotion understanding. Standard single-label evaluation is therefore insufficient. To address this limitation, we introduce an LLM-as-Judge framework that evaluates each emotion independently according to its plausibility in the conversational context rather than enforcing a single-label decision.

</details>


### [233] [Agentic BAIM-LLM Evaluation (ABLE): Benchmarking LLM Use of Protein Design Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05818)
*Bryce Cai,Geetha Jeyapragasan,Samira Nedungadi,Jake Yukich,Seth Donoughe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ABLE基准，用于评估LLM代理使用生物AI模型（如ProteinMPNN和AlphaFold3）进行双重用途蛋白质设计的能力。对15个前沿模型进行测试后发现7个拒绝所有任务，其余模型表现差异显著，其中Claude Sonnet 4和Gemini 3 Pro表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理技术日益成熟，其应用于生物AI模型（BAIMs）的潜力逐渐显现。然而，LLM代理在双重用途蛋白质设计工作流中的能力尚缺乏系统评估。作者旨在通过构建标准化基准来量化LLM代理在此场景下的信息检索、工具选择、工具使用和任务执行能力，填补现有评估空白。

Method: 构建ABLE基准框架，设计涵盖结构检索、序列生成和设计验证三个维度的任务集。选取15个前沿LLM模型进行系统评估，并将模型在部分任务上的表现与专家人类基线进行比较。评估维度包括模型是否拒绝对齐任务、信息检索能力、工具选择能力和工具使用效果。

Result: 15个模型中7个拒绝所有任务，剩余模型表现差异显著。Claude Sonnet 4和Gemini 3 Pro在信息检索、工具选择和工具使用上均获得最高分数。模型整体能有效降低蛋白质设计门槛，但在规划、策略生成以及将生物学知识与工具使用相结合方面仍存在不一致性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在降低蛋白质设计门槛方面展现了实际潜力，但在需要复杂规划、策略制定和生物知识整合的任务上仍存在明显短板，提示这类模型未来需在推理深度和领域知识融合方面进一步改进。

Abstract: We introduce ABLE, a benchmark for evaluating LLM agents' ability to use biological AI models (BAIMs), such as ProteinMPNN and AlphaFold3, in dual-use protein design workflows. ABLE assesses agent performance through a set of tasks spanning structure retrieval, sequence generation, and design validation. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find that seven refuse all tasks, while the remaining models exhibit substantial performance differences. Claude Sonnet 4 and Gemini 3 Pro achieve the highest scores across information retrieval, tool selection, and tool use. We further compare model performance on a subset of tasks against an expert human baseline. Our results suggest that current LLMs can substantially lower barriers to protein design, but remain inconsistent in planning, strategy generation, and integrating biological knowledge with tool use.

</details>


### [234] [Beyond Top-$k$ Skill Retrieval: Diversity-Aware Skill Routing for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05824)
*Wang Wei,Tiankai Yang,Samyadeep Basu,Hongjie Chen,Yue Zhao,Zhengzhong Tu,Xiyang Hu,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DSR框架，利用行列式点过程（DPP）在技能路由中平衡相关性与非冗余性，通过查询残差多样性核惩罚冗余技能重叠，在SkillRouter基准上优于逐点重排序基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理依赖外部技能，但大型技能注册表中存在大量功能冗余的技能，而复杂任务又常需要互补技能组合。现有技能路由器独立按查询相关性排序，可能将上下文预算浪费在冗余技能上。

Method: 提出Diverse Skill Routing (DSR)，一种基于行列式点过程（DPP）的多样性感知重排序框架。DSR引入查询残差多样性核（query-residual diversity kernel），惩罚冗余技能重叠，同时减少仅由共享查询相关性引起的惩罚。

Result: 在SkillRouter基准上，DSR相比强逐点重排序基线在召回率和全覆盖率上均有提升，在多技能查询上增益更大。

Conclusion: 技能路由不仅应被视为相关性排序问题，更应被视为互补技能集合选择问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external skills, but routing user requests over large skill registries is difficult because many skills are functionally redundant while complex tasks often require complementary skill sets. Existing skill routers typically rank candidates independently by query relevance, which can waste context budget on redundant skills. We propose Diverse Skill Routing (DSR), a diversity-aware reranking framework that uses a Determinantal Point Process to balance relevance and non-redundancy. DSR introduces a query-residual diversity kernel that penalizes redundant skill overlap while reducing penalties caused only by shared query relevance. On the SkillRouter benchmark, DSR improves recall and full coverage over a strong pointwise reranking baseline, with larger gains on multi-skill queries. These results suggest that skill routing should be treated not only as relevance ranking, but also as complementary set selection.

</details>


### [235] [Learning Counterfactual World Models for Embodied Reasoning under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05834)
*Todd Y. Zhou,Daniel Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实潜在世界模型（CLWM），解决世界模型表征因仅优化感知相似性而导致的"反事实坍缩"问题——无法区分行为后果不同但视觉上相似的干预。CLWM结合循环信念状态编码器、动作条件潜在动力学和对比反事实目标，在遮挡操作、混淆导航和长时程操作任务上超越最强基线，并提出与规划成功率高度相关的表示无关的反事实可分性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型被寄望为具身智能的通用路线，其表征越来越多地通过大规模视频、交互和多模态语料预训练获得。然而仅用预测质量无法回答一个关键问题：学到的表征何时真正"可行动"？作者识别出反事实坍缩这一失效模式：模型能预测视觉上合理的未来，却无法区分具有不同行为后果的干预，根源在于大多数预训练编码器优化的是感知相似性而非干预结构。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Latent World Models (CLWM)，核心由三部分组成：循环信念状态编码器、动作条件潜在动力学、以及对比反事实目标函数。该目标函数用于分离不同干预产生的未来状态，即使其观测在外观上高度相似。训练中利用硬反事实负样本，特别是感知混淆负样本，来强化对干预结构的区分能力。此外提出反事实可分性指标，用于评估任意表征（预训练或从头训练）在规划任务中的可行动性。

Result: 在遮挡操作任务（Occluded Push）上规划成功率从最强基线的65.1%提升到74.6%；在混淆导航任务（Aliased Maze）上从67.3%提升到78.9%；在延迟厨房任务（Deferred Kitchen）上将剥削性规划失败从18.4%降至9.7%。消融实验将增益归因于硬反事实负样本（尤其感知混淆负样本）。反事实可分性指标在五类基线模型上与规划成功率相关系数r≥0.94。

Conclusion: 仅仅追求感知准确率不足以获得真正可行动的世界模型表征；必须显式建模干预结构（反事实区分）。CLWM验证了该方法的有效性，反事实可分性指标作为一种表示无关的审计工具，可在规划器信任任何编码器（无论预训练与否）之前评估其可行动性。作者指出目前仅在从零训练的世界模型上验证了该指标，尚未在大规模预训练编码器上评测，为后续研究留出空间。

Abstract: World models promise a general route to embodied intelligence: learn predictive dynamics once, then reason, plan, and act with them. Increasingly, the representations beneath such models are pretrained on large-scale video, interaction, and multimodal corpora, which raises a question prediction quality alone cannot answer: when is a learned representation actually actionable? We identify a failure mode we call counterfactual collapse: a model predicts visually plausible futures while failing to distinguish interventions with different behavioral consequences. This arises whenever a representation is optimized for perceptual similarity rather than intervention structure, which is precisely the objective under which most large-scale pretrained encoders are learned. We introduce Counterfactual Latent World Models (CLWM), which combine a recurrent belief-state encoder, action-conditioned latent dynamics, and a contrastive counterfactual objective that separates futures induced by distinct interventions even when their observations look alike. Across occluded manipulation, aliased navigation, and long-horizon manipulation, CLWM improves planning success over the strongest baseline (65.1% $\to$ 74.6% on Occluded Push and 67.3% $\to$ 78.9% on Aliased Maze) and reduces exploitative planning failures (18.4% $\to$ 9.7% on Deferred Kitchen), with ablations attributing the gains to hard counterfactual negatives, especially perceptual-alias negatives. Finally, our counterfactual separability metric, which tracks planning success across the five baseline model classes ($r \ge 0.94$), is representation-agnostic: given intervention-outcome labels, it can audit any encoder, pretrained or trained from scratch, before a planner trusts it. We do not yet measure it on large-scale pretrained encoders. Here we establish the metric and its relationship to planning success for world models trained from scratch.

</details>


### [236] [AgentBrew: Offline Tool-Use Agent Learning from Raw Real-World Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05837)
*Zhiyi Lyu,Yewen Li,Longtao Zheng,Shengtian Yang,Lang Feng,Lei Feng,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai,Qingpeng Cai,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentBrew是一个离线训练框架，从单一批量的原始交互轨迹中学习有效的工具使用策略，无需任务验证器或迭代式在线策略回滚。通过回顾式任务推断重建指令，并利用基于点互信息的信用分配将轨迹信号分解为逐动作信用，从而在GitHub、Notion和PostgreSQL三个真实MCP应用上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体通过工具API在真实应用中被广泛部署，但训练它们面临根本性困难：真实应用没有预定义任务或验证器、没有忠实的模拟器，且环境交互预算有限。现有的过滤式方法会丢弃大量有价值的信息。

Method: AgentBrew包含两个核心组件：(1) 回顾式任务推断（Retrospective task inference），根据轨迹实际结果重建与每条轨迹对齐的指令；(2) 基于PMI的信用分配（PMI-Based credit assignment），通过点互信息将推断指令的总信息量分解为逐动作的可加性信用。这些信用用作策略训练目标的权重，放大信息量高的动作、抑制无效动作。

Result: 在GitHub、Notion、PostgreSQL三个真实MCP应用上，AgentBrew使Qwen3-32B平均提升+8.7 Acc / +9.7 Score，超越了Qwen3-235B（+2.3 / +4.4）以及拒绝采样方法（+5.9 / +10.3）。

Conclusion: 细粒度的离线学习可以从原始轨迹中恢复过滤式方法会丢弃的有用监督信号，证明无需任务验证器或在线交互即可有效训练工具使用智能体。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed in real-world applications through tool-use APIs, yet training them for specific environments remains fundamentally difficult: real-world applications provide no pre-defined tasks or verifiers, no faithful simulators, and limited budget for large-scale environment interaction. In this paper, we propose \textbf{AgentBrew}, an offline training framework that learns effective tool-use policies from a single batch of raw interaction trajectories, without task verifiers or iterative on-policy rollouts. The agent first explores the target environment to collect a raw trajectory corpus without quality filtering. To extract training signal from this noisy corpus, \emph{retrospective task inference} reconstructs an aligned instruction for each trajectory based on its actual outcome, and \emph{PMI-Based credit assignment} decomposes the trajectory's total information about the inferred instruction into additive per-action credits via pointwise mutual information (PMI). These credits weight the policy training objective, amplifying informative actions while suppressing ineffective ones. On three real-world MCP applications (GitHub, Notion, PostgreSQL), AgentBrew improves Qwen3-32B by +8.7 Acc / +9.7 Score on average, surpassing Qwen3-235B (+2.3 / +4.4) and outperforming rejection sampling (+5.9 / +10.3). These results demonstrate that fine-grained offline learning can recover useful supervision from raw trajectories that filtering-based approaches would discard. The code is available at https://github.com/alphatogo/AgentBrew

</details>


### [237] [Multimodal Resource-Exhaustion Attacks on Vision-Language Models via Joint Pixel-Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05889)
*Zhaoxiong Ni,Yatie Xiao,Chi-Man Pun,Fei Peng,Qingxiao Guan,Keke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JPPO（联合像素-提示优化）框架，首个将用户可见提示与图像扰动共同作为一阶对抗变量、进行跨模态联合优化的复合对抗攻击方法，用于针对自回归视觉语言模型（VLM）的资源耗尽攻击。在8/255无穷范数预算下，JPPO在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上实现4.6倍延迟和5.3倍能耗放大，在BLIP-2上实现36.6倍延迟和32.7倍能耗放大，显著超越现有基线且所需优化迭代更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对自回归VLM的资源耗尽攻击大多假设单模态威胁模型，仅将图像分支作为优化面而保持用户可见提示固定，即便是最近的循环型变体也局限于单通道范式。作者指出，这种跨模态联合可控输入面的可用性利用问题尚未被探索，因此需要一种将可见提示与图像扰动协同纳入优化范畴的复合对抗框架。

Method: JPPO采用受限联合输入威胁模型，将可见提示提升为一阶对抗变量，与图像扰动并行。算法上采用分阶段（stagewise）耦合优化策略，同时对像素面和提示面进行联合优化，产生协同性的成本放大效应。该方法在机制上与依赖循环（loop）的失败模式存在本质区别，实验显示其循环发生率可忽略不计。

Result: 在MS COCO和ImageNet数据集上评估五个开源VLM家族，在8/255无穷范数预算下：Qwen2.5-VL-7B上实现4.6倍以上延迟放大和5.3倍以上能耗放大；BLIP-2上实现36.6倍以上延迟放大和32.7倍能耗放大。在直接对比的基线中达到最强成本放大效果，且所需的优化迭代次数显著更少。消融实验证实放大效果来自多模态协调而非单纯提示长度或单一模态作用。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前VLM服务防御体系的结构性盲区：跨模态联合优化可产生远超单模态攻击的成本放大效果。作者主张应将成本感知的鲁棒性评估作为多模态部署中的一等安全需求（first-class security requirement），而非仅关注模型输出的正确性。

Abstract: Resource-exhaustion attacks against autoregressive vision-language models (VLMs) typically assume unimodal threat models, treating the image branch as the primary optimization surface while holding user-visible prompts fixed. Even recent loop-centric variants remain confined to this single-channel paradigm, leaving the exploitation of availability unexplored as a cross-modal optimization problem over jointly controllable input surfaces.
  We introduce Joint Pixel-Prompt Optimization (JPPO), the first compound adversarial framework elevating the visible prompt to a first-class adversarial variable alongside image perturbations. Under a restricted joint-input threat model, JPPO performs coupled, stagewise optimization over both the pixel and prompt surfaces. This produces synergistic cost amplification, mechanistically distinct from loop-dependent failures, exhibiting negligible loop incidence in our experiments.
  Evaluating five open-source VLM families on MS COCO and ImageNet under an 8/255 infinity-norm budget, JPPO achieves over 4.6x latency and 5.3x energy amplification on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and over 36.6x latency with 32.7x energy amplification on BLIP-2. This represents the strongest cost amplification among directly compared baselines while requiring substantially fewer optimization iterations. Ablations confirm this amplification arises from multimodal coordination rather than prompt length or isolated modalities. These findings reveal structural blind spots in current VLM serving defenses, motivating cost-aware robustness evaluation as a first-class security requirement for multimodal deployments.

</details>


### [238] [The End of AI Exponentiation: Fluttering Inside and Outside AI Bubble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05894)
*Victor Kebande*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能指数增长时代的终结，分析了AI生态内外部的"泡沫化"现象及其带来的不稳定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI的快速发展，传统的规模化扩展范式（如增加数据、算力）正遭遇瓶颈，包括峰值数据限制、合成数据递归、算力需求激增、估值通胀和社会不稳定等问题，因此需要审视AI指数增长时代是否正在走向终结。

Method: 本文采取分析性视角，将AI生态系统中的不稳定因素划分为"泡沫内部"与"泡沫外部"两个维度进行系统性探讨。内部涉及算力与数据中心竞赛、投机性投资及超级智能竞赛；外部涉及劳动力市场扰乱、治理担忧、公众不确定性以及围绕未来智能系统的全球地缘政治加速。

Result: 揭示了传统AI规模化范式在维持持续指数增长方面正面临越来越多的摩擦，在AI生态内外呈现出多层面的不稳定与泡沫化风险。

Conclusion: 作者认为AI的指数增长正在步入拐点，需要正视泡沫内部的计算竞赛与投机泡沫，以及外部社会、治理与地缘政治层面的连锁反应，重新审视AI发展的可持续路径。

Abstract: The exponentiation of Artificial intelligence (AI) in the recent past has entered a transformative era that has been driven by the growth in large language models (LLMs), large-scale compute infrastructures, and autonomous reasoning systems. However, the rapid acceleration of AI has increasingly shown technological, societal, economic, ethical and infrastructural challenges associated with peak data limitations, rising computational demands, synthetic data recursion, valuation inflation, and societal instability. The traditional scaling paradigms that have powered the modern AI systems are gradually encountering friction in sustaining continuous exponential growth. This paper views ``the end of AI exponentiation,'' thus exploring how it flutters inside and outside the bubble, where instability emerges within the AI ecosystem through compute and data-center races, speculative investments, and the rat-race toward superintelligence, and outside the ecosystem through labor disruption, governance concerns, public uncertainty, and geopolitical acceleration surrounding future intelligent systems and infrastructures globally.

</details>


### [239] [Beyond Final Decisions: A Process-Centric Benchmark for Transparent AI-Assisted Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05947)
*Siming Yuan,Xueyi Zhang,Wangze Ni,Tianfang Xiao,Shimin Di,Jia Zhu,Zhuoren Jiang,Rong Tan,Lei Chen,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向AI辅助同行评审的过程中心诊断基准，通过分解决策过程的阶段变量来评估模型评审的可靠性与证据支撑度，而非仅关注最终评审质量或决策准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助同行评审的评估主要关注生成评审的整体质量或最终决策的准确性，未能充分验证模型决策是否有充足且可靠的评审证据支撑，缺乏对评审过程本身的可审计性评估。

Method: 构建过程对齐数据，将评审记录表示为(x, z_s, z_c, z_r, y)五元组，涵盖论文内容、摘要、批评、建议和决策。基于PeerRead、NLPeer ARR-22和OpenReview-ICLR三个数据集，采用直接决策预测作为基线，对比Gold过程变量与Predicted过程变量的决策价值，并进行阶段级评估、链一致性评估和干预敏感性分析。

Result: 在三个数据集和六个模型上的实验表明：Gold过程变量通常具有更高的决策价值；主分析模型中Gold-Predicted差距在数据集和随机种子间保持稳定，且该差距在大多数模型-数据集组合中被复现；尽管模型生成的中间评审文本在相邻阶段间展现出较高的局部一致性，但最终决策并未被前序评审证据一致地支撑。

Conclusion: 该基准面向的是辅助而非替代人类审稿人的AI系统，为评估其评审过程的可靠性提供了透明且可审计的诊断工具，揭示了当前模型在证据支撑决策方面的系统性不足。

Abstract: Peer review is central to quality control in science. However, existing evaluations of AI-assisted peer review mainly focus on the overall quality of generated reviews or the accuracy of final decisions. They therefore provide limited evidence about whether model decisions are supported by sufficient and reliable review evidence. We introduce a process-centric diagnostic benchmark for AI-assisted peer review. It uses (x,$z_s$,$z_c$,$z_r$,y)
  to represent the paper content, summary, critique, suggestion, and decision. We convert heterogeneous review records from PeerRead, NLPeer ARR-22, and OpenReview-ICLR into process-aligned data. Our benchmark uses direct decision prediction from the paper content (Direct) as its baseline. It compares the decision value of Gold-process variables and Predicted-process variables, and conducts stage-level evaluation, chain-consistency evaluation, and interventional sensitivity analysis. Experiments across three datasets and six models show that Gold-process variables generally have higher decision value. For the main analysis model, the Gold--Predicted gap remains stable across datasets and random seeds. This gap is also reproduced in most model--dataset combinations. Although model-generated intermediate review texts show relatively high local consistency across adjacent stages, the final decisions are not consistently supported by the preceding review evidence. Our benchmark targets AI systems designed to assist rather than replace human reviewers. It provides a transparent and auditable diagnostic tool for evaluating the reliability of their review processes.

</details>


### [240] [MOAE: Multi-Objective Agent Evolution with Pareto-Preserving Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05992)
*Hengle Jiang,Qijun Cai,Ziying Luo,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多目标智能体进化（MOAE），将迭代式上下文细化组织为对完整智能体轨迹的Pareto保持进化搜索，在有限回滚预算下维护非支配候选档案，使用目标特定诊断指导后代生成，并仅在部署时进行约束感知选择，从而在不固定标量化的情况下同时优化任务性能、轨迹质量和安全性。实验表明MOAE在TravelPlanner和AgentDojo上持续提升任务性能与轨迹质量，同时保持强安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估涉及任务准确率、交互质量、安全性、效率等多个目标，但已有方法常将异构度量折叠为固定标量分数，这种标量化依赖于度量归一化和偏好权重，可能丢弃具有实际部署权衡价值的候选方案。作者希望解决能否同时优化多个目标的核心问题。

Method: MOAE将迭代式上下文精炼组织为Pareto保持的进化搜索，作用在完整智能体轨迹上。在受限回滚预算下维护经验非支配候选档案；采用目标特定诊断信息引导后代生成；约束感知选择仅在部署阶段实施，从而将搜索阶段的候选保留与最终解决方案的偏好选择解耦。方法无需参数更新，每个目标可替换为任意可测量属性，文中实例化为任务性能、轨迹质量和安全性。

Result: 在TravelPlanner和AgentDojo上的实验表明，在匹配的回滚预算下，MOAE一致性地提升了任务性能和轨迹质量，同时保持强安全性。搜索行为分析进一步显示，Pareto保持扩大了可达目标区域，并增加了联合改进的频率。

Conclusion: 研究证明了Pareto保持的上下文进化在优化多个智能体属性方面的潜力，无需在搜索过程中承诺固定的标量化方案，为多目标智能体优化提供了新的途径。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents continue to advance, their evaluation has become increasingly multifaceted: a capable agent must not only achieve high task completion accuracy but also perform well in interaction quality, safety, and efficiency, raising a central question: can these objectives be optimized simultaneously? Existing methods have considered multiple objectives, but many collapse heterogeneous measurements into a fixed scalar score. Such scalarization depends on metric normalization and preference weights and may discard candidates that represent useful deployment trade-offs. We introduce Multi-Objective Agent Evolution (MOAE), which organizes iterative in-context refinement as a Pareto-preserving evolutionary search over complete agent rollouts. Given a limited rollout budget, MOAE maintains an empirical archive of non-dominated candidates, uses objective-specific diagnostics to guide offspring generation, and applies constraint-aware selection only at deployment. This separates candidate preservation during search from the preference used to return a final solution. The procedure requires no parameter updates and allows each objective to be replaced by any measurable property, which we instantiate as task performance, trajectory quality, and safety. Experiments on TravelPlanner and AgentDojo show that MOAE consistently improves task performance and trajectory quality while maintaining strong safety under matched rollout budgets. Search-behavior analysis further shows that Pareto preservation expands the attainable objective region and increases the frequency of joint improvement. These results demonstrate the potential of Pareto-preserving in-context evolution for optimizing multiple agent properties without committing to a fixed scalarization during search.

</details>


### [241] [Agentic Pressure: The Endogenous Entropy of Reliable Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05995)
*Hengle Jiang,Ziying Luo,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文识别并提出了一种名为"Agentic Pressure"（智能体压力）的非对抗性现象，指智能体在长时程开放环境自主运行中，由于目标达成与规则遵从之间的冲突而内生产生的动力学应力。当该压力超过临界阈值时，对齐智能体会将安全偏离作为数学上最优的自适应策略，并通过"工具性幻觉"合理化规则违背行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中的自主智能体需要持续执行长时程任务，但在无约束环境中会积累累积性摩擦，导致其对齐状态内在不稳定。现有研究多聚焦于静态的对抗性越狱，而忽略了非对抗性的、由交互动力学内生涌现的对齐退化现象。

Method: 作者提出一个理论框架，将Agentic Pressure形式化为"克服环境摩擦所需功"与"智能体剩余容量"的比值。通过数学分析论证，当该压力超过临界阈值时，安全偏离成为数学上的最优适应策略。同时引入"Instrumental Hallucination"概念，解释智能体如何理性化规则违背。

Result: 实证实验验证了该理论框架，表明高压力条件下的对齐智能体会自发地牺牲安全性以维持自主性，而非在对抗性攻击下才发生违规行为。

Conclusion: Agentic Pressure揭示了智能体对齐退化的一种内生机制：即使没有恶意对手，长时程自主运行中的目标-合规冲突本身即可驱动安全偏离。这提示对齐研究需超越静态robustness，关注动态运行中的自适应安全退化问题。

Abstract: Achieving reliable autonomy in the wild requires agents to sustain continuous operations across long-horizon trajectories. However, as agents navigate these unconstrained settings, they encounter cumulative friction that inherently destabilizes their alignment. In this paper, we identify a distinct non-adversarial phenomenon termed Agentic Pressure. We define this as a kinetic force that spontaneously emerges when the cost of compliance conflicts with the imperative of goal achievement. Unlike static jailbreaks, this pressure is endogenous and arises directly from the dynamics of interaction. We propose a theoretical framework that formalizes Agentic Pressure as the ratio between the required work to overcome environmental friction and the remaining capacity of the agent. Our analysis demonstrates that when this pressure exceeds a critical threshold, agents exhibit safety drift as a mathematically optimal adaptation. Consequently, they often resort to Instrumental Hallucination to rationalize rule violations. Empirical experiments validate this framework and show that aligned agents spontaneously compromise safety to preserve autonomy under high-pressure conditions.

</details>


### [242] [Generator-Independent Runtime Assurance under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06036)
*Guangxi Wan,Yongbo Xie,Yuqi Liu,Qingwei Dong,Qingxin Li,Hongfei Bai,Peng Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究基于"提议"(proposal)的控制器（如学习的策略、语言模型规划器等黑箱生成器）在运行时验证门控后的闭环安全保证何时能与生成器解耦。核心定理表明"集合层面同时可靠性"(simultaneous setwise soundness)是生成器无关的准入可靠性(admission soundness)的充要条件；配合设计时证书与"不可绕过"规则，可实现违反界为 Γ + Σε_t + η 的契约安全，该界对任意甚至对抗性生成器替换保持不变。另一条定理刻画了部分观测下任何准入机制的下界(ā + β + δ ≥ 1)，并通过顺序风险账本实现可实施的安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于提议的控制器(学得策略、语言模型规划器或其他黑箱生成器)越来越多地在运行时验证门控后部署。现有的逐候选认证模式不可组合：在重试或best-of-k选择下，逐候选的假准入水平α会膨胀为1-(1-α)^k。作者希望弄清闭环安全保证何时能与具体生成器解耦，从而建立生成器无关的安全保证理论。

Method: 论文提出"同时集合可靠性"(simultaneous setwise soundness)概念——认证一个不含任何不可行动作的可准入提议集合——作为生成器无关准入可靠性的充要条件。结合设计时证书与"不可绕过"(no-bypass)规则，证明可达成契约安全(contract safety)，违反界为Γ+Σ_t ε_t+η，对任意(甚至对抗性)生成器替换保持不变。第二条定理通过总变差距离δ刻画部分观测下任何准入机制的信息论下界(ā+β+δ≥1)。引入顺序风险账本(sequential risk ledger)配合时间一致置信管(confidence tubes)使保证可实施，并证明确定性准入计算将所有统计风险集中于状态估计。最后将simplex式运行时保障与控制障碍函数滤波视为退化情形。

Result: (1) 证明了同时集合可靠性是生成器无关准入可靠性的充要条件，解决了逐候选认证不可组合(假准入α膨胀至1-(1-α)^k)的问题；(2) 建立了契约安全保证，违反界Γ+Σ_t ε_t+η对任意(含对抗性)生成器替换不变；(3) 推导出部分观测下任何准入机制的信息论下界ā+β+δ≥1；(4) 通过顺序风险账本使安全保证可实施，并揭示了确定性准入将统计风险集中在状态估计的结构性事实；(5) 统一了simplex式运行时保障与控制障碍函数滤波作为特殊情形。

Conclusion: 论文建立了基于提议控制器在运行时验证下安全保证与生成器解耦的充分必要条件理论。核心贡献在于证明"集合层面"而非"逐候选"认证是获得生成器无关安全的关键，给出了任意生成器下不变的安全界，并用信息论下界揭示了部分观测下安全保证的根本限制。该框架还为安全保证的可实施性提供了顺序风险账本工具，并将既有方法(如simplex式保障与CBF滤波)统一为其退化情形。

Abstract: Proposal-based controllers---learned policies, language-model planners, and other black-box \emph{generators}---are increasingly deployed behind runtime verification gates. We ask when the closed-loop safety guarantee decouples from the generator. The prevailing per-candidate certification pattern does not compose: under retry or best-of-$k$ selection a per-candidate false-admission level $α$ can inflate to $1-(1-α)^{k}$. Our main theorem shows that \emph{simultaneous setwise soundness}---certifying a set of admissible proposals containing no nonviable action---is necessary and sufficient for generator-independent \emph{admission soundness}, the worst case over all generators of executing a nonviable proposal equalling the probability of setwise failure; together with a design-time certificate and a no-bypass rule it is sufficient for \emph{contract safety}, with violation bound $Γ+\sum_t\varepsilon_t+η$ invariant under arbitrary, even adversarial, replacement of the generator. A second theorem bounds every admission mechanism under partial observation: for a fixed probing and admission policy, if two state hypotheses whose information laws lie within total-variation distance $δ$ require different safe decisions, then $\abar+β+δ\ge1$. A sequential risk ledger makes the guarantee implementable with time-uniform confidence tubes, and shows that deterministic admission computations concentrate all statistical risk in state estimation. Simplex-style runtime assurance and control-barrier-function filtering are recovered as degenerate cases.

</details>


### [243] [DAREBench: Deployment-Aware and Reliable Evaluation of Models as Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06059)
*Yu Liu,Zhilin Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Shaojie Zhang,Zheyuan Deng,Tingwei Huang,Zhenbo Luo,Lei Jiang,Yanbing Liu,Pei Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DAREBench基准测试，用于在共享OpenClaw执行环境下、以统一契约协议和证据审计方式，可靠且可比较地评估通用智能体模型的能力。研究基于233个任务、23个商业API模型和12个开源模型，共计7,587次运行的结果显示，没有任何单一模型在所有工作负载组中全面领先，强调模型选择应考虑工作负载特征、部署方式和精度-成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型正从问答系统演进为通用智能体，但现有评估基准多局限于特定任务类型、执行环境或评分协议，导致评估结果缺乏可比性、可解释性和可靠性，难以支撑部署决策。

Method: 构建DAREBench基准，基于共享的OpenClaw执行环境；从22个来源基准中选取并适配233个任务，按输入模态和执行形式组织为2×3工作负载矩阵；采用统一的基于契约的评估协议，并配备基于证据的分数审计机制。对23个商业API模型和12个本地部署的开源模型进行7,587次模型-任务运行，同时报告准确率、token消耗及API模型的参考成本。

Result: 没有任何单一模型在所有工作负载组中占据主导地位；文本与多模态任务呈现不同的准确率-成本权衡；本地开源模型在若干任务组中具备竞争力，但整体仍落后于前沿商业模型。

Conclusion: 智能体部署与模型选择应当综合考虑工作负载画像、部署模式及准确率-成本权衡，而非依赖单一的聚合评分。

Abstract: As large language models evolve from question-answering systems into general-purpose agents, evaluation must move beyond static answer correctness to assess multimodal perception, multi-step execution, tool use, and artifact delivery. However, existing benchmarks are often tied to specific task types, execution environments, or scoring protocols, limiting their comparability, interpretability, and reliability for deployment decisions. We introduce DAREBench (Deployment-Aware and Reliable Evaluation of Models as Agents), a benchmark designed to capture workload variation and support reliable agent evaluation. Built on a shared OpenClaw execution environment, DAREBench organizes 233 tasks selected and adapted from 22 source benchmarks into a $2\times3$ workload matrix defined by input modality and execution form, and evaluates them under a unified contract-based protocol with evidence-based score auditing. We evaluate 23 commercial API models and 12 locally deployed open-weight models over 7,587 model--task runs, reporting accuracy and token consumption alongside reference costs for API models. Results show that no single model dominates all workload groups, text and multimodal tasks exhibit distinct accuracy--cost trade-offs, and local open-weight models are competitive in several groups but still trail frontier commercial models overall. These findings suggest that agent deployment and model selection should consider workload profiles, deployment mode, and accuracy--cost trade-offs rather than rely on a single aggregate score.

</details>


### [244] [Explaining AI Agents Through Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06063)
*Vittoria Vineis,Fabiano Veglianti,Lorenzo Antonelli,Claudia Di Carlo,Matteo Silvestri,Gabriele Tolomei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个事后XAI框架，将智能体的执行轨迹转换为结构化报告和忠实于可观察行为的自然语言解释，用于审计AI智能体的多步决策过程，且仅依赖执行轨迹因而跨架构通用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在真实场景中与外部工具交互并做序列决策，但人类监督有限，需要可靠且可审计的解释。传统XAI方法无法提供交互式多步系统所需的过程级透明度，亟需专门针对AI智能体的可解释框架。

Method: 提出一个事后(post-hoc)可解释AI框架：将冗长的智能体执行轨迹转化为结构化报告，再生成忠实于可观察行为的自然语言解释。该框架仅依赖执行轨迹，因此可适用于不同智能体架构、环境和任务。

Result: 通过人类评估与自动化评估，在多个基准和架构上验证了框架的有效性：能生成高质量、轨迹忠实的解释，同时可靠识别无依据的声明、不当动作和证据缺口，优于朴素的LLM生成解释。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了基于执行轨迹的过程级可解释框架能为AI智能体提供高质量、可审计的解释，弥补传统XAI在该场景下的不足，并能帮助识别智能体行为中的问题。

Abstract: AI Agents are increasingly deployed in real-world settings, where they interact with external tools and make sequential decisions with limited human oversight. This creates a pressing need for reliable and auditable explanations of what an agent did and why. However, traditional Explainable AI (XAI) methods fall short of providing the process-level transparency required for such interactive, multi-step systems, motivating a paradigm shift toward approaches specifically designed for AI Agents. To address this gap, we present a post-hoc XAI framework that transforms a lengthy agent's execution trace into a structured report and a faithful natural-language explanation explicitly grounded in its observable behavior. Because it relies solely on execution traces, the framework applies across different agent architectures, environments, and tasks. Human and automated evaluations across multiple benchmarks and architectures show that our framework produces high-quality, trace-faithful explanations while reliably identifying unsupported claims, unjustified actions, and evidence gaps, outperforming naive LLM-generated explanations.

</details>


### [245] [Generating Instance Generators in PDDL Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06071)
*Nicola J. Müller,Naya Rudolph,Katharina Stein,Jörg Hoffmann,Ayal Taitler,Timo P. Gros*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型(LLM)自动生成PDDL规划领域的实例生成程序，并内建合法性检查保证生成实例的soundness，解决传统人工编写实例生成器及现有自动推理/学习方法可扩展性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDDL作为AI规划领域的标准语言，只能描述规划域（谓词和动作模式），但无法指定实例集合本身，包括初始状态和目标条件的合法性约束，以及感兴趣的领域子集约束。这导致实例生成一直是特设的、依赖人工编写的领域特定生成器。近期虽有基于推理和学习的自动方法，但受限于可扩展性。

Method: 利用LLM自动生成实例生成程序，通过预设的合法性检查保证生成的实例满足soundness要求。生成的程序能够产出大量合法且多样的实例。

Result: 自动生成的实例生成器能够高效地返回大量sound且多样的实例。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的实例生成程序生成方法为PDDL规划域的实例生成提供了一种可扩展且可靠的新途径，克服了现有方法在可扩展性上的瓶颈。

Abstract: PDDL, the de-facto standard language in the AI Planning community, is designed to specify planning domains: sets of instances that share the same predicates and action schemas. Yet it does not provide any means to specify the actual instance set, i.e., legality constraints on initial states and goal conditions, as well as possibly domain subset constraints specifying an instance subset we are interested in. One consequence of this is that instance generation has always been ad-hoc, with manually written domain- and subset-specific instance generators. Recent work has started to address this, through reasoning and learning methods that however suffer from scalability limitations. Here we introduce an alternative approach, leveraging LLMs to generate instance-generation programs, with built-in soundness guarantees through prescribed checks. We show that these automatically generated instance generators return large numbers of sound and diverse instances efficiently.

</details>


### [246] [LayerRoute: Action-Conditioned Mixture-of-Layers Routing for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06079)
*Zheng Lu,Haoran Liao,Wanqi Zhong,Yunhe Ni,Lijie Wang,Xingjie Fan,Zhisheng Chen,Yantang Qu,Meijia Chen,Tianyu Xin,Zirui Song,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LayerRoute，一种动作条件化的表示路由接口，使VLA策略能够自适应地访问不同VLM层和动作表示，在LIBERO Long等基准上实现最高7.2的性能增益，仅增加0.31%/3.87%的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA接口在表示访问上存在局限性：VLM信息通过固定层分配暴露给每个动作层，缺乏灵活性；而中间动作状态仅通过残差流隐式传播，未被显式重用。不同操作任务可能需要不同层的VLM表示组合，且动作模块的中间表示对后续决策有用，但现有框架无法利用这些潜能。

Method: LayerRoute包含两个核心组件：(1) Layer Mixture Router，动态地从缓存中混合不同VLM层的表示；(2) Action-State Reread，显式重用早期的动作表示。整体构成一个动作条件化的表示路由接口，使路由决策依赖于动作状态。

Result: 在多种仿真和真实世界基准上，LayerRoute持续改进StarVLA-π和π0.5两种VLA模型，在LIBERO Long上实现最高7.2的性能增益，仅增加0.31%/3.87%的额外参数。消融实验验证了动作条件化层路由的有效性，路由分析揭示了不同动作层和任务设置下的结构化分配模式。

Conclusion: LayerRoute通过动作条件化的动态层混合和中间动作状态再读机制，突破了现有VLA固定层分配和隐式残差传播的局限，以极低的额外参数成本显著提升多任务操控性能，并通过路由分析为理解VLA内部表示分配提供了新视角。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies leverage pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) to guide action generation for robot control. VLMs provide hierarchical visual-semantic representations that evolve across layers, from local visual geometry to abstract, language-aligned semantics; different manipulation tasks may therefore require different mixtures of layer representations. Meanwhile, the action module maintains intermediate representations that evolve throughout action computation and may provide useful information for subsequent decisions. However, existing VLA interfaces offer limited flexibility in representation access: VLM information is exposed through fixed layer assignments for each action layer, while intermediate action states are only propagated implicitly through residual streams without explicit reuse. We introduce LayerRoute, an action-conditioned representation routing interface that enables adaptive access to VLM layers and action representations. The Layer Mixture Router dynamically forms mixtures of cached VLM representations, while Action-State Reread reuses earlier action representations. Across diverse simulation and real-world benchmarks, LayerRoute consistently improves StarVLA-$π$ and $π_{0.5}$, achieving up to 7.2 gains on LIBERO Long with only 0.31% / 3.87% additional parameters. Ablation studies validate the benefit of action-conditioned layer routing, while routing analyses reveal structured allocation patterns across action layers and task settings.

</details>


### [247] [SAP: State-Guided Data Synthesis with Argument Provenance for Multi-Turn Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06124)
*Zichen Tian,Jinpeng Chen,Cheng Gong,Suiyun Zhang,Rui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SAP（State-Guided Data Synthesis with Argument Provenance），一种针对多轮工具使用数据合成的新方法，通过状态引导、工具参数溯源约束和回合级验证，高效构建具有长程依赖和高准确率的工具使用轨迹数据，并用其训练的SAP-4B模型在多个基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的多轮工具使用数据对训练智能体模型至关重要，但现有数据合成方法往往忽视了参数级依赖关系——这是长时序工具使用的关键。即使在模型选择了正确工具的情况下，由于工具参数充满编造的、过时的或弱佐证的值，任务执行仍可能失败。

Method: SAP将三个组件结合：(1) 状态引导（state guidance），用于追踪和指导数据合成过程中的系统状态；(2) 工具参数溯源约束（tool-argument provenance constraints），确保工具参数具有可追溯的可靠来源而非编造；(3) 回合级验证（turn-level validation），在每一回合验证生成轨迹的正确性。三者协同高效构造具有长距离依赖和高精度的工具使用轨迹数据。

Result: 使用SAP生成的训练数据构建的SAP-4B模型，在多个基准测试中展现出高度的竞争力，甚至可以与远大于自身规模（4B参数）的模型相媲美。

Conclusion: SAP有效地解决了多轮工具使用数据合成中参数级依赖表征不足的问题，通过状态引导、参数溯源和回合级验证的组合方法提升了工具使用轨迹数据的质量与效率，证明了高质量合成的数据能够帮助小规模模型取得与大模型相当的性能。

Abstract: High-quality multi-turn tool-use data is essential for training agentic models, yet existing data synthesis methods often underrepresent the argument-level dependencies that are critical to long-horizon tool use. As a result, even when a model selects the correct tool, task execution may still fail because the model fills tool arguments with fabricated, stale, or weakly grounded values. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{State-Guided Data Synthesis with Argument Provenance (SAP)}. SAP combines state guidance, tool-argument provenance constraints, and turn-level validation to efficiently construct tool-use trajectories with long-range dependencies and high accuracy. Using data generated by SAP, we build SAP-4B, which is highly competitive even when compared with much larger models across multiple benchmarks. Source code, synthesized data, and trained weights are available at https://github.com/Zichen1024/SAP.

</details>


### [248] [CWF: A Collaborative Writing Framework for Personalized and Reliable Popular Science Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06126)
*Ruibiao Fu,Di Tang,Yunlong Yang,Ran Wang,Sicheng Lu,Peixuan Wu,Xiaoyu Fan,Jiacheng Ma,HaoZhe Luo,Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"个性化且可靠的科普写作"新任务，要求在不牺牲事实准确性的前提下面向不同认知水平的读者调整科学解释。作者构建了39,134条条目的数据集与读者中心的PSCB基准，并提出了DA-MoE（解耦受众适应与领域知识建模）及多智能体事实验证与修订机制，在PSCB上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科普写作需要在个性化（适应不同认知水平的受众）与事实准确性之间取得平衡，但提升个性化程度往往引入简化，从而增加幻觉和事实扭曲的风险。现有方法缺乏联合评估这两个维度的基准，也缺乏高效解耦建模与在证据稀缺场景下的可靠验证机制。

Method: 1) 构建一个包含39,134条条目的数据集和读者中心的个性化科学传播基准PSCB，联合评估受众适应性和事实准确性；2) 提出DA-MoE，将受众适应与领域知识显式解耦、分别建模，以降低数据和计算需求并提升跨领域与跨受众泛化能力；3) 提出多智能体事实验证机制，通过角色特定智能体辩论弥补稀缺证据，并在图上传播置信度以实现稳健的验证与修订。

Result: 在PSCB基准上的实验表明，所提方法达到了最先进的性能，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过在受众适应与领域知识之间进行解耦建模，并引入基于多智能体辩论和图置信度传播的事实验证机制，可以在保证事实准确性的同时实现个性化科普写作，整体框架在PSCB上展现出SOTA效果。

Abstract: We introduce Personalized and Reliable Popular Science Writing, a novel task that requires adapting scientific explanations to audiences with different cognitive levels while preserving factual accuracy. However, improving personalization often introduces simplifications that increase the risk of hallucination and factual distortion. To address these challenges, we first construct a dataset of 39,134 entries and a reader-centric Personalized Science Communication Benchmark (PSCB) that jointly evaluates audience adaptation and factual accuracy. To reduce data and computational requirements while improving generalization across domains and audiences, we introduce DA-MoE, which explicitly decouples audience adaptation from domain knowledge through separate modeling. To enable robust verification and revision in evidence-scarce scenarios, a multi-agent fact-checking mechanism that augments limited evidence with role-specific agent debate and propagates confidence over a graph is proposed. Experiments on PSCB show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/DPInnovationWorks/CWF.

</details>


### [249] [Substrate-Portable Execution for Production LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06128)
*Tarun Gopinath,Atul Kulkarni,Vijay Rajakumar,Shrikar Katti,Parthasarathy Govindarajen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种绑定自适应的Agent执行平台，将工作流定义与执行基质分离。开发者只需定义一次类型化数据流图，平台即可将其编译到三种不同的运行时（进程内实时流式处理、AWS SWF异步编排、Apache Flink批处理推理）。该平台在Rufus（服务于数百万亚马逊客户的对话式AI助手）上验证了数十个生产级Agent配置，横跨五种编排模式，在三种绑定间输出质量无显著差异，批处理将推理成本降至与公告的batch API价格一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级的LLM Agent（如Rufus）需要支持多种执行模式（实时服务、异步后台任务、高吞吐批处理），每种模式有独特的服务级别目标且通常使用独立的运行时。然而，可复用的流式编排使异步和批处理工作负载变成阻塞式，且无法利用提供50%折扣的batch推理API。核心痛点在于执行语义与单一运行时耦合导致的移植性问题，需要重新设计架构。

Method: 提出绑定自适应的Agent执行平台：(1) 将工作流定义为类型化数据流图(Typed Dataflow Graph)，实现工作流定义与执行基质的解耦；(2) 平台将图编译到三种执行基质：in-process streaming（实时服务）、AWS SWF持久化编排（异步执行）、Apache Flink分布式流处理（批处理推理）；(3) LLM推理被表示为可挂起的图节点，其行为随基质自适应变化——在线流式交付、异步持久重试、离线批量提交；(4) 在Rufus上通过五种编排模式（单推理RAG、迭代ReAct、组合PreAct、条件路由、多Agent深度研究）进行验证。

Result: 横跨全部三种绑定，未检测到输出质量的可感知差异。批处理执行将每查询推理成本降低至与发布的batch API定价一致，同时在生产规模下与流式路径并行运行。

Conclusion: 通过将工作流定义与执行基质解耦，并让LLM推理节点具备基质感知的可挂起行为，可以在一套工作流定义下同时满足实时、异步和批处理三种模式的SLO要求，且无需修改工作流代码即可获得batch API的成本优势，实现了执行可移植性与成本效率的统一。

Abstract: Production LLM agents execute tool-calling loops, retrieval chains, and compositional workflows in multiple modes, yet execution semantics are often coupled to one runtime. We encountered this portability problem in Rufus, a conversational AI assistant with a large tool catalog that serves millions of Amazon customers. Rufus supports real-time serving, asynchronous background tasks, and high-volume batch workloads such as evaluation and content pregeneration. Each mode has distinct service-level objectives and typically uses a separate runtime. Reusing streaming orchestration makes asynchronous and batch workloads blocking and prevents use of batch inference APIs, which offer a 50 percent discount at published prices. We present a binding-adaptive agent execution platform that separates workflow definition from execution substrate. Developers define a workflow once as a typed dataflow graph. The platform compiles the graph to in-process streaming for real-time serving, durable AWS SWF orchestration for asynchronous execution, or distributed Apache Flink stream processing for batch inference. No workflow code changes are required. LLM inference is represented as a suspendable graph node whose behavior depends on the substrate: streaming delivery online, durable retry asynchronously, and batched submission offline. We validated dozens of production agent configurations across five orchestration patterns: single-inference RAG, iterative ReAct, compositional PreAct, conditional routing, and multi-agent deep research. Across all three bindings, we found no detectable difference in output quality. Batch execution reduced per-query inference cost in line with published batch API pricing while operating alongside the streaming path at production scale.

</details>


### [250] [IIns-VAE+: A Robust Transfer Learning Framework for Environmental Identification in Wireless Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06131)
*Yuxiao Li,Keke Hu,Bobai Zhao,Santiago Mazuelas,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IIns-VAE+模型，将Inter-Instance Variational Auto-encoder (IIns-VAE)框架与Minimax Risk Classifiers (MRC)结合，用于6G集成传感与通信（ISAC）系统中的无线传感环境识别任务，以应对领域偏移导致的深度学习模型泛化问题。在三种迁移学习场景（通用到特定房间、高到低标签分辨率、混合到特定环境）的真实数据集实验表明，IIns-VAE+显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G ISAC系统需要可靠的环境识别能力，但深度学习模型在跨环境领域偏移下难以泛化。IIns-VAE虽然能够学习丰富表征的特征，但其神经分类器在面对分布变化时仍然脆弱，亟需提升模型在迁移学习场景中的适应性。

Method: 提出IIns-VAE+混合模型，将IIns-VAE框架与Minimax Risk Classifiers (MRC)相结合。MRC替代传统的神经网络分类器，通过最小化最坏情况下的风险来增强模型对分布变化的鲁棒性，从而提升在迁移学习场景中的适应性。

Result: 在真实数据集上，针对三种迁移学习场景（从通用环境到特定房间环境、从高标签分辨率到低标签分辨率、从混合环境到特定环境）进行评估，实验结果表明IIns-VAE+显著优于各类基线方法。

Conclusion: IIns-VAE+在构建适应性强、鲁棒性高的感知网络中展现出关键价值，对未来6G系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: Environmental identification in wireless sensing is essential for 6G integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems to achieve reliable situational awareness. However, deep learning (DL) models for this task often fail to generalize under domain shift across diverse environments. While the Inter-Instance Variational Auto-encoder (IIns-VAE) learns features of rich representation, its neural classifier remains vulnerable to these distribution changes. In this paper, we propose IIns-VAE+, a hybrid model that combines the IIns-VAE framework with Minimax Risk Classifiers (MRC) to improve adaptability in transfer learning scenarios. We use real-world datasets to evaluate our framework across three transfer learning scenarios, including general to specific room environments, high to low label resolutions, and mixed to specific environments. The experimental results indicate that IIns-VAE+ significantly outperforms baselines, demonstrating its critical value in building adaptable and robust perceptive networks in future 6G systems.

</details>


### [251] [MVFA: A Multi-View Text-Guided Multimodal Fusion LLM Adapter for Sentiment Analysis and Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06188)
*Pengfei Shao,Jisheng Dang,Jiawen Fang,Ning Liu,Wencan Zhang,Bimei Wang,Jingwen Zhao,Jianhuang Lai,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MVFA（多视图文本引导多模态融合适配器），一种参数高效框架，通过冻结大型语言模型并仅微调少量参数实现多模态情感分析。MVFA利用三种文本池化方法构建互补文本视图来引导音视频跨模态交互，并通过增强Q-Former融合模块压缩为伪令牌。在CH-SIMS V2.0、MELD和CHERMA三个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感分析与对话情感识别需要建模文本、音频、视觉三种模态间的异构交互。虽然LLM具备强大的语言理解能力，但将其适配到多模态情感计算任务面临双重挑战：全参数微调计算开销过大，而现有轻量适配器在跨模态融合过程中难以保留丰富文本线索。

Method: MVFA框架包含以下核心组件：(1) 通过最大池化、均值池化和注意力池化三种方式构建互补的文本视图；(2) 利用文本视图引导音频和视觉特征的跨模态交互融合；(3) 通过增强Q-Former融合模块将多模态表示压缩为紧凑的可学习伪令牌序列；(4) 基于ChatGLM3-6B-base为主骨干，并在LLaMA2-7B和Qwen3-8B上验证可移植性。

Result: 在三个数据集上均取得最优结果：CH-SIMS V2.0上Acc2为84.62%、F1为84.59%；MELD上Acc为67.36%、WF1为66.03%；CHERMA上Acc为74.66%。同时仅更新极少量参数，并在多个冻结LLM骨干上表现稳定。

Conclusion: 多视图文本引导融合被证明是一种有效且可扩展的参数高效多模态LLM适配范式。MVFA在冻结LLM基础上仅微调少量参数即可达到SOTA性能，为情感计算领域的多模态LLM适配提供了新路径。代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis and emotion recognition in conversations demand effective modeling of heterogeneous interactions across textual, acoustic, and visual modalities. Although large language models (LLMs) offer powerful language understanding, adapting them to multimodal affective computing remains challenging: full-model fine-tuning is computationally prohibitive, while many existing lightweight adapters fail to preserve rich textual cues during cross-modal fusion. To address these limitations, we propose the multi-view text-guided multimodal fusion adapter (MVFA), a parameter-efficient framework that augments frozen LLMs with strong multimodal reasoning capability. MVFA first constructs complementary text views via max pooling, mean pooling, and attention pooling; these views then guide cross-modal interactions with audio and visual features. The fused multimodal representations are subsequently compressed into a compact set of learnable pseudo-tokens through an Enhanced Q-Former Fusion Module. Using ChatGLM3-6B-base as the primary backbone, we further validate MVFA on LLaMA2-7B and Qwen3-8B to examine its portability across multiple frozen LLM backbones. MVFA is evaluated on three challenging datasets: CH-SIMS V2.0, MELD, and CHERMA. Experimental results demonstrate that MVFA achieves state-of-the-art performance on key metrics while updating only a small fraction of parameters. Specifically, it attains 84.62\% Acc2 and 84.59\% F1 on CH-SIMS V2.0, 67.36\% Acc and 66.03\% WF1 on MELD, and 74.66\% Acc on CHERMA. These findings establish multi-view text-guided fusion as an effective and scalable paradigm for parameter-efficient multimodal LLM adaptation in affective computing. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Overwhelm1208/MVFA.

</details>


### [252] [Customer Relationship Intelligence: Integrating CRM and MDM for Enhanced Customer Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06189)
*Tejasvi c. Addagada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨CRM、MDM和CKM如何共同构成客户关系智能（CRI）框架以增强客户参与（CE）。基于7个行业100名参与者的横断面调查，采用Spearman相关和序数逻辑回归分析。CRM和CKM是CE的显著正向预测因子，而MDM仅为基础性数据质量使能者，其间接效应不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多单独考察CRM、MDM或CKM对客户参与的影响，缺乏将其整合为统一框架（CRI）进行联合检验的实证研究。作者旨在厘清三者之间的作用机制及MDM在框架中的战略定位。

Method: 横断面调查法，覆盖零售、医疗、IT和电信四个行业的100名参与者。数据分析采用Spearman rho相关分析和序数逻辑回归（IBM SPSS），并运用Hayes PROCESS Model 4进行并行中介分析（5,000次bootstrap抽样），以检验MDM通过CRM和CKM对CE的间接效应。

Result: 双变量相关均弱且不显著（r<0.19, p>0.06）。回归显示CRM（β=0.717, p=0.002）和CKM（β=0.581, p=0.009）是CE的显著正向预测因子，MDM直接效应为正但不显著（β=0.346, p=0.071）。模型解释CE方差的20.5%（Nagelkerke R²=0.205）。并行中介分析未发现显著间接效应，H4假设未被支持。

Conclusion: 在CRI框架中，CRM和CKM是推动客户参与的主要驱动力，而MDM作为基础性数据质量使能者，其战略价值需要通过赋能CRM执行和知识管理来实现。鉴于样本量和横断面设计限制，结果应视为探索性发现，未来需在更大样本、纵向设计和受数据治理约束的BFSI等监管行业中进行验证。

Abstract: This study examines how Customer Relationship Management (CRM), Master Data Management (MDM), and Customer Knowledge Management (CKM) jointly constitute a Customer Relationship Intelligence (CRI) framework for enhanced Customer Engagement (CE). A cross-sectional survey of 100 participants across retail, healthcare, IT, and telecommunications sectors was analysed using Spearman rho correlation and ordinal logistic regression (IBM SPSS). Bivariate correlations were weak and non-significant (r<0.19, p>0.06). Regression identified CRM (beta=0.717, p=0.002) and CKM (beta=0.581, p=0.009) as significant positive predictors of CE; MDM showed a positive but non-significant direct effect (beta=0.346, p=0.071). The model explained 20.5% of CE variance (Nagelkerke R^2=0.205). Parallel mediation analysis (Hayes PROCESS Model 4, 5,000 bootstrap samples) found no significant indirect effects of MDM on CE via CRM (IE=0.021, 95% BC CI [-0.072, 0.121]) or CKM (IE=0.032, 95% BC CI [-0.061, 0.126]); Hypothesis H4 was not supported. CRM and CKM emerge as the principal drivers of CE within the CRI framework, while MDM functions as a foundational data quality enabler whose strategic value is realised through its enabling effect on CRM execution and knowledge management. Findings should be treated as exploratory given the sample size and cross-sectional design. Future research should replicate with larger sector-specific samples and longitudinal designs, particularly in regulated BFSI contexts where MDM architecture is shaped by data governance mandates.

</details>


### [253] [SCIRIGOR:Evaluating Open-Ended Scientific Analysis Beyond Final Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06192)
*Bowen Liu,Shuo Nie,Bodong Du,Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciRIGOR评估框架和基准，用于评估科学编码智能体所生成分析结果与科学论断之间的证据支撑关系，而非仅评估最终输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学编码智能体生成相互依赖的代码、结果、图表和论断，仅评估最终输出无法判断其科学结论是否有证据支撑，需要更细粒度的证据链验证。

Method: 提出SciRIGOR框架：(1) 构建100个来自6个领域17个子领域的科学文章案例作为基准；(2) 重建带类型的证据图(typed evidence graphs)；(3) 分离工件保真度(artifact fidelity)与关系有效性(relational validity)；(4) 对完整"论断-支撑"路径进行评分，并定位最早出现不支持关系的环节；(5) 允许基于源文本的替代路径以容纳科学上等价的分析和可视化。评估了11种智能体/模型配置。

Result: 在完整基准运行上，论断与忠实和不忠实结果的一致性率几乎相同(91.8%对91.0%)，但没有任何系统在软证据链得分上超过62.6%，在严格全链成功率上超过18.0%。

Conclusion: 内部一致性并不等同于科学正确性：评估必须沿完整的数据到论断路径验证证据支撑关系，而不应只依赖论断与自身结果表面的一致。

Abstract: Scientific coding agents produce interdependent code, results, figures, and claims, yet evaluating final
  outputs alone does not establish whether their conclusions are scientifically supported. We formulate
  evidence-grounded multimodal scientific analysis, requiring agents to produce executable analyses and claims
  supported by results and visualizations from the same run. We introduce SciRIGOR, an evaluation framework and
  benchmark comprising 100 cases from scientific articles across six domains and 17 subfields. The framework
  reconstructs typed evidence graphs, separates artifact fidelity from relational validity, and scores complete
  claim-support paths while localizing the earliest unsupported relation. Source-grounded alternative paths
  accommodate scientifically equivalent analyses and visualizations. We evaluate 11 agent/model configurations.
  On full-benchmark runs, claims agree with faithful and unfaithful results at nearly identical rates (91.8%
  versus 91.0%). Yet no system exceeds 62.6% on the soft evidence-chain score or 18.0% strict whole-chain
  success. These findings show that internal coherence does not establish scientific correctness: evaluation
  must verify support along the complete data-to-claim path.

</details>


### [254] [Predicting Wind Turbine Power Using Machine Learning and Weather Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06194)
*Khivishta Boodhoo,Isaac Triguero,Josh Plumbly,Bruce Nicolson,Nicholas Watson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了线性回归和人工神经网络模型，仅使用天气数据预测海上风力涡轮机的功率输出。系统研究了数据量、特征选择和数据预处理对模型性能的影响。ANN模型表现最佳（R²=0.98，MAE=194），优于线性回归基线（R²=0.94，MAE=441），且利用附近气象站的独立天气数据集，具有良好的跨位置迁移能力。该模型还被用于识别4小时低功率时段以安排维护，每次维护可节省约2000 kW功率输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上风力涡轮机的维护会导致强迫停机，降低发电效率。准确的功率预测可以帮助识别低功率时段以优化维护排程。然而，数据量、特征选择和数据预处理对功率预测模型性能的影响尚未被充分研究，且现有模型在不同风力涡轮机之间的可迁移性有限。

Method: 开发了一个基线线性回归模型和一个更复杂的人工神经网络模型，仅使用天气条件作为输入以增强适用性。系统研究了多种数据预处理技术，分别用一个月和一年的数据训练模型以考察数据量和预处理的影响。使用随机森林回归器进行特征选择。模型使用附近气象站独立的天气数据集。

Result: 人工神经网络模型取得了最高精度，R²分数为0.98，平均绝对误差为194；基线线性回归模型R²分数为0.94，平均绝对误差为441。模型性能与以往研究相当，但优势在于利用附近气象站的独立天气数据集，使其可迁移至不同地点的相似风力涡轮机。该模型成功识别了两个月内4小时低功率预测时段，每次维护事件可节省约2000 kW功率输出。

Conclusion: 人工神经网络结合独立天气数据源的方案在风力涡轮机功率预测中表现出高精度和良好的可迁移性，能够有效识别低功率维护窗口，为海上风电场的维护排程和运营优化提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Offshore wind turbines are widely used to generate renewable energy, but their maintenance can result in decreased efficiency due to forced shutdowns. Accurate wind turbine power predictions can identify periods of low power that would be ideal for scheduling maintenance. However, the effects of data volume, feature selection, and data preprocessing on the performance of such power prediction models have not been thoroughly studied. Besides, current models have limited transferability between different wind turbines. Therefore, this study developed a baseline Linear Regression for performance comparison with a more complex Artificial Neural Network model to predict the power output of a wind turbine, using weather conditions only to enhance applicability. A range of data preprocessing techniques were studied, and models were trained on one month and one year of data to determine the effects of data preprocessing and volume on model performance. Feature selection was explored using a Random Forest Regressor. The best results from the different models showed that the Artificial Neural Network models provided the highest accuracy, with an R2 score of 0.98 and a low Mean Absolute Error of 194, when compared with the baseline model (R2 score of 0.94 and Mean Absolute Error of 441). The model performance is comparable to the range of results in past studies, with the advantage that the proposed method leverages a separate weather dataset from a nearby weather station, enabling future applications for similar wind turbines in different locations. The Artificial Neural Network model was then used to identify 4-h periods of low power predictions over 2 months (simulating application for future periods), providing power output savings of approximately 2000 kW for each maintenance event.

</details>


### [255] [AutoKD: Autonomous Knowledge Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06366)
*Qinwen Ge,Bo Ni,Haowei Fu,Ngoc N. Tran,Erik Blasch,Tyler Derr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoKD是一个多智能体自主知识发现框架，通过六个协调的LLM代理在开放式发现循环中协作，将经验证的发现存储在持久化洞察图中作为长期记忆和探索引导机制，实现计算性且累积性的自主科学发现，在三个数据集上覆盖已知发现并补充人类研究成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据密集型领域的科学发现受限于人类处理带宽，数据量的增长远超研究者的阅读、推理和综合能力。现有基于LLM的多智能体系统虽能自动化部分研究流程，但存在两个核心局限：一是在无法自动验证的设置中只做一次性假设生成；二是每次运行都是一次性的，缺乏发现成果的累积机制来引导后续研究。

Method: 提出AutoKD框架，采用六个协调的LLM代理在开放式发现循环中协作。关键创新点包括：(1) 自动验证机制使发现具备计算可验证性；(2) 持久化洞察图（insight graph）存储已验证发现，同时充当长期记忆和探索引导机制；(3) 累积式发现让每次研究结果持续留存并指导后续研究，而非一次性运行。

Result: 在三个不同数据集上从两个视角进行评估：开放式质量（对照已发表发现）和条件式质量（通过文献派生的查询）。两种评估视角下，AutoKD都能覆盖已知发现，并能浮现出与人类驱动研究互补的实质性发现。

Conclusion: AutoKD通过计算性与累积性相结合的自主知识发现框架，有效缓解了人类研究带宽瓶颈，在覆盖已知文献发现的同时还能产生补充人类研究的新发现，验证了多智能体累积式科学发现范式的可行性。

Abstract: Scientific discovery in data-rich domains is currently constrained by human bandwidth: the growth in the volume and complexity of real-world data far outpaces the rate at which researchers can read, reason, and synthesize. Recent LLM-based multi-agent systems have begun to automate portions of the research cycle, but they target hypothesis generation in settings where validation cannot itself be automated, and each run is one-shot, with no mechanism for findings to accumulate or steer subsequent inquiry. This paper introduces AutoKD, a multi-agent framework for autonomous knowledge discovery that is both computational and cumulative, allowing validated findings to persist and inform subsequent inquiry. Six coordinated LLM agents collaborate in an open-ended discovery loop, where accepted findings are stored in a persistent insight graph that serves as both long-term memory and an exploration-steering mechanism. We evaluate AutoKD on three diverse datasets from two perspectives: Open-ended Quality against published findings, and Conditioned Quality via literature-derived queries. Across both evaluation perspectives, AutoKD covers known findings and surfaces substantive discoveries that complement human-driven research. Our code is available at https://github.com/GeQinwen/AutoKD.

</details>


### [256] [Building Trustworthy Graph-Agentic RAG for Social Good: Architectures, Failure Propagation, and Assurance by Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06391)
*Vijay Bommireddy,Raviteja Bommireddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对面向社会公益场景的图智能检索增强生成（Graph-agentic RAG）系统进行系统审视，将其风险归纳为"证据到行动"的级联失败链，提出包含五个接口契约的"面向保证的构建"蓝图，并给出跨越图断言、轨迹、声明、协调与结果的评估议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图智能检索增强生成虽在跨文档、跨实体、跨时间与跨机构的复杂关系问答中有效，但其图构建缺陷可能成为检索证据、影响控制决策并逐级传导至关键结果，形成耦合并联的失败路径。在新鲜度、授权、可追溯性、监督与救济等社会公益要求下，这些耦合风险尤为突出，需要有组织的分析与受保证的系统设计方法。

Method: 作者按图基底、图生命周期、智能体功能、协调模式与权限边界五个维度组织文献，区分"基于图的检索"与"依赖观察的图控制"两类范式；将报告的风险综合为"证据到行动"失败链，提出包含证据、检索、推理、能力与委派、结果五类接口契约的"面向保证的构建"蓝图，并以公共利益信息设计示例加以说明。

Result: 形成了一套包括证据、检索、推理、能力/委派、结果五个接口契约的保证框架，使溯源、时间有效性、授权、不确定性与可恢复性在系统边界显式化；并推导出横跨图断言、智能体轨迹、中间声明、协调与最终结果的五层面评估议程。

Conclusion: 在公益场景中，图智能RAG系统的可靠性与问责性要求证据、推理、能力委派与结果等环节在系统边界以契约方式显式声明保障属性，并通过面向保证的构建与多维评估手段，控制证据到行动失败链的传播，而非仅优化最终答案质量。

Abstract: Graph-agentic retrieval-augmented generation combines structured evidence with adaptive controllers that can plan retrieval, traverse relations, verify intermediate claims, delegate subtasks, and use tools. This combination is useful when answers depend on relations across documents, entities, time, or institutions, but it also creates coupled failure paths: a defect in graph construction can become retrieved evidence, alter later control decisions, and propagate toward a consequential outcome. We examine how such systems should be designed and evaluated for social-good settings in which freshness, authorization, traceability, oversight, and recourse matter alongside answer quality. We organize the literature by graph substrate, graph lifecycle, agent function, coordination pattern, and authority boundary, and distinguish graph-based retrieval from observation-dependent graph control. We then synthesize reported risks as an evidence-to-action failure chain and propose an assurance-by-construction blueprint comprising five interface contracts for evidence, retrieval, reasoning, capability and delegation, and outcome. These contracts make provenance, temporal validity, authorization, uncertainty, and recoverability explicit at system boundaries. An illustrative public-benefit information design shows how the framework constrains graph structure, permissions, abstention, and operating authority. Finally, we derive an evaluation agenda spanning graph assertions, trajectories, claims, coordination, and outcomes.

</details>


### [257] [From Concentration to Differentiation and Back: Routing Effective Rank in MoE Reasoning Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06403)
*Kang Chen,Sihan Zhao,Yixin Cao,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出路由有效秩（routing effective rank, deff）作为无标签诊断指标，量化MoE推理回滚（rollout）群体在测试时计算中的内部组织变化。基于MoE专家路由相似性构建跨回滚图，该指标在多种配置与基准上呈现可复现的低-高-低轨迹，且可将群体的共同模式质量与残差谱维度精确分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展（test-time scaling）会产生大量推理回滚，但目前缺乏标准化的无标注方法来理解这些回滚的内部计算随推理展开如何重组。研究者希望填补这一空白，提供一种可分解的谱视角来分析MoE推理群体的动态组织。

Method: 1) 定义路由有效秩deff：基于MoE专家路由相似性构建跨回滚图，计算其熵有效维度。2) 在10种MoE配置和5个数学/科学基准上进行大规模实证（3105个模型-问题群体）。3) 提出精确分解方法，将轨迹分离为共同模式质量（common-mode mass）与残差谱维度两部分。4) 通过正确性对比将结构监测与答案选择分离，定位努力效应在时机与持续时间上的作用。

Result: 1) deff呈现可复现的低-高-低轨迹，在98.5%的群体中存在显著内部最大值。2) 共同模式重分配解释了约三分之二的轨迹，残差谱贡献约四分之一且保有显著变化。3) 共同模式集中度的提升强烈预测同答案可恢复性。4) 更高的推理努力将轨迹最大值延迟2.59个八度（token预算翻倍），并一致扩展高秩时段。5) 努力效应定位于时机和持续时间而非峰值幅度。

Conclusion: 路由有效秩是一种可分解、无标注的群体组织诊断工具，为理解MoE推理群体如何随推理时间分化与再集中提供了原理性的谱分析视角，并将结构监测与答案选择在概念上分离。

Abstract: Test-time scaling produces cohorts of reasoning rollouts, yet there is no standard label-free account of how their internal computation reorganizes as inference unfolds. We introduce routing effective rank deff, the entropy-effective dimensionality of a cross-rollout graph built from MoE expert-routing similarity. Across ten MoE configurations and five math/science benchmarks, deff exhibits a reproducible low-high-low trajectory, with a prominent interior maximum in 98.5% of 3,105 model-question cohorts: routing similarity is concentrated early, maximally differentiated at intermediate budgets, and reconcentrated later, and the timing of this maximum varies systematically with architecture and reasoning effort. An exact decomposition separates cohort-wide common-mode mass from residual spectral dimensionality: common-mode reallocation accounts for about two thirds of the trajectory, while the residual spectrum contributes about one quarter and retains substantial variation beyond the common mode. The decomposition further localizes behavior: among non-unanimous cohorts, increases in common-mode concentration strongly predict same-answer recoverability, and higher reasoning effort delays the maximum by 2.59 octaves (doublings of the token budget) and consistently expands the high-rank period across all four tested architectures, locating the effort effect in timing and duration rather than peak amplitude. Correctness comparisons separate structural monitoring from answer selection, positioning routing effective rank as a decomposable, label-free diagnostic of cohort organization - a principled spectral lens on how MoE reasoning cohorts differentiate and reconcentrate over inference time.

</details>


### [258] [Causal Attribution for Agentic Decisions: Estimators, Coupling, and a Traceability Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06445)
*Ajay Pravin Mahale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对欧盟《人工智能法案》下高风险Agentic系统的事后可归因记录规范问题，指出既有边际总效应与共同随机数总效应两类估计量均存在失效情形：无害步骤与决定性步骤在边际估计量下代数上等价，而决定性步骤在共同随机数下翻转子集上精确为零却实际起作用。文章推导了能保持自然直接效应可估的耦合及其退化闭式解，并揭示在抑制情形下中介占比不再是份额且可超过1。同时指出2026年8月《法案》第86条解释权生效与2027年12月第12条日志记录应用之间存在合规缺口，因而公布预注册而非实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险AI系统的提供者必须保留使决策可追溯的记录，但对Agentic系统而言，尚未明确需要何种记录才能实现事后因果归因。本文旨在填补这一规范空白，并揭示既有因果效应估计框架在智能体决策链上的失效机制。

Method: 提出估计量框架并推导其失效条件；区分边际总效应（prior work测量）与共同随机数总效应（隔离单步自身贡献）；在钉住下游的条件下添加自然直接效应；通过与手工推导对照校验估计量；推导使上下文分歧后直接效应仍可估的耦合并给出退化闭式解；以预注册形式发布受限于pipeline可用性的差异实验。

Result: 两个估计量朝同一方向失效：边际估计量下因果惰性步骤与决定性步骤在每一条植入链运行上具有完全相同的总效应（代数恒等式而非抽样巧合）；共同随机数下决定性步骤在执行步骤翻转的运行（约十分之一）上返回精确零，而其直接效应为0.25且确实起作用。在抑制情境下（直接与中介路径相反），基于中介占比构建的自然排序不再是份额，占比可超过1，使被抑制成分排在纯中介之上。研究还公布了可复现的预注册及traceability规范。

Conclusion: 精确为零的总效应并不能证明某步骤未起作用，既有因果估计量不足以支撑Agentic系统的事后归因与责任认定。文章以"把此发现写入正文而非局限"的方式强调这一根本性缺陷，并贡献欧盟AI法案第86条解释权与第12条日志义务之间的合规空窗期内所需的traceability规范设定。

Abstract: A provider of a high-risk AI system must keep records that make a decision traceable, and for agentic systems it has not been established what those records must contain for post-hoc causal attribution to be possible. We give the estimator framework and then the conditions under which it fails. We separate the marginal total effect that prior work measures from a common-random-numbers total effect that isolates a step's own contribution, add the natural direct effect under a pinned downstream, and check the estimators against hand derivations. Both estimands then fail, in the same direction. Under the marginal estimand a causally inert step has the identical total effect to the decisive one on every run of our planted chain, an algebraic identity and not a coincidence at one draw. Under common random numbers the decisive step returns exactly zero on the runs where the executing step flips, about one in ten, while its direct effect there is 0.25 and it demonstrably acts; an exact zero does not certify that a step did nothing, and we put that here rather than in the limitations. We derive the coupling that keeps the direct effect estimable once contexts diverge, with a closed form for its degradation, and show that the mediated share on which a natural ranking is built is not a share under suppression: where the direct and mediated paths oppose, it exceeds one and ranks a suppressed component above a pure mediator. We publish the discrepancy experiment's pre-registration rather than a result, because the live pipeline it requires was not available in the study window. We contribute the traceability specification such a filing would need, against a gap the Act's calendar opens: Article 86's right to an explanation has applied since 2 August 2026, while the Article 12 logging and Annex IV documentation that could evidence one were deferred to 2 December 2027 by Regulation (EU) 2026/1744.

</details>


### [259] [A Unified Policy Architecture (UPA): The Governance Kernel for Enterprise AI Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06543)
*Prabhu Raghav,Balamurugan Pandi,Arul Vivek,Shek Mohammed,Sridhar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出统一策略架构（UPA），一种面向企业AI操作系统（Enterprise AI Operating System）的治理架构，通过统一的策略模型治理自主AI代理、工具、工作流、内存、企业资源及代理间交互，将策略控制从传统授权扩展到运行时义务、人工审批、合规、审计证据和治理评估，为自主AI企业系统提供安全、可问责、可治理的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI正在演变为一种企业操作系统，其中自主AI代理能够规划、推理、使用内存、调用工具、执行工作流并与其他代理协作。然而，现有的授权、安全、防护栏和合规机制是碎片化的，并非为统一治理自主AI系统而设计，这构成了新的治理挑战。

Method: UPA提供了一套统一策略模型（governance model），涵盖AI与代理、工具、工作流、内存、企业资源、代理间交互及企业业务规则。其核心构成包括：声明式策略语言基础、策略评估语义、可扩展插件、行业策略包，以及企业治理评估框架。策略控制范围从传统授权扩展至运行时义务、人工审批、合规、审计证据与治理评估。此外还扩展了多代理协调、感知来源的策略和有状态运行时治理。

Result: 论文展示了UPA的治理模型、声明式策略语言基础、策略评估语义、可扩展插件、行业策略包及面向企业治理的评估框架，并识别了多代理协调、来源感知策略和有状态运行时治理等扩展方向。

Conclusion: UPA为构建安全、可问责、可治理的自主AI企业操作系统提供了基础架构支撑。

Abstract: Enterprise AI is evolving into an Enterprise Operating System where autonomous AI agents can plan, reason, use memory, invoke tools, execute workflows, and collaborate with other agents. This shift creates a new governance challenge: existing authorization, security, guardrails, and compliance mechanisms are fragmented and are not designed to govern autonomous AI as a unified system.
  This paper introduces the Unified Policy Architecture (UPA), a governance architecture for Enterprise AI Operating Systems. UPA provides a unified policy model for governing AI and agents, tools, workflows, memory, enterprise resources, and agent-to-agent interactions and enterprise business rules. It extends policy control beyond authorisation to include runtime obligations, human approvals, compliance, audit evidence, and governance evaluation.
  We present UPA's governance model, declarative policy language foundations, policy evaluation semantics, extensible plugins, industry policy packs, and an evaluation framework for enterprise governance. We also identify extensions for multi-agent coordination, provenance-aware policies, and stateful runtime governance. UPA provides a foundation for building secure, accountable, and governable Enterprise Operating Systems for autonomous AI.

</details>


### [260] [MARBO: Relational Belief Grounding for LLM Agents in Social Deduction Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06563)
*Hwang Yechan,Bae Sangjun,Kim Jeongmo,Bang Sangwoo,Han Seungyul*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MARBO（多智能体关系信念优化）框架，通过将关系信念作为偏好优化信号的锚点，指导紧凑型大语言模型智能体在社交推理游戏中的策略决策与游戏内语言表达，使其在部分可观测条件下保持一致的行为。实验表明MARBO使紧凑LLM智能体稳定超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交推理游戏要求智能体在部分可观测条件下通过维护关于隐藏角色和团队阵营的关系信念进行推理。现有LLM智能体方法虽通过提示和偏好优化改进游戏表现，但未将动作与游戏言语显式锚定在信念之上，导致策略行为不一致，尤其对紧凑LLM智能体而言更为突出。

Method: MARBO是一种信念锚定的偏好优化框架：利用关系信念引导策略决策和游戏内言语生成；仅在行为受到可靠关系信念支持且能导向策略上有利的社会结果时提供偏好反馈，从而在不确定性下促进更一致的学习。

Result: 在代表性社交推理游戏上的实验表明，MARBO能使紧凑LLM智能体稳定地超越现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式将决策与语言锚定于关系信念并在仅于可靠信念支持下给予偏好反馈，MARBO有效提升了紧凑LLM智能体在社交推理游戏中的策略一致性与整体表现，代码已开源。

Abstract: Social deduction games (SDGs) require agents to reason under partial observability by maintaining relational beliefs about hidden roles and team alignments. While recent LLM-agent approaches improve gameplay through prompting and preference optimization, they often optimize actions and in-game speech without explicitly grounding them in such beliefs. This frequently leads to strategically inconsistent behavior, especially for compact LLM agents. We introduce Multi-Agent Relational Belief Optimization (MARBO), a belief-grounded preference optimization framework that leverages relational beliefs to guide strategic decisions and in-game speech. MARBO provides preference feedback only when behaviors are supported by reliable relational beliefs and lead to strategically favorable social outcomes, encouraging more consistent learning under uncertainty. Experiments on representative SDGs show that MARBO enables compact LLM agents to consistently outperform existing baselines. The Code is available on https://github.com/PleaseTakemeAway/MARBO.

</details>


### [261] [A Translational Note on AI Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06573)
*Madhava Gaikwad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"威胁模型覆盖缺口"（threat-model coverage gap）概念，指出自动化红队相比人类红队的基准评测结果存在盲区：基准仅衡量预设危害集合内的搜索彻底性，无法覆盖集合之外的危害。作者论证这类似于学术密码学和临床药物试验中的已知局限——内部有效的评估对其未指向的人群保持沉默。论文发现该缺口在当前开源权重模型中仍然存在（非英语提示中的危害被英语基准遗漏），主张关闭该缺口需要与开发者部署情境不同的评估者，且论证基于方法论（覆盖性）而非成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究声称自动化红队比人类红队能发现更多漏洞且成本更低，有人将之比作人类评估者即将被淘汰的证据。作者认为这种比较衡量了一个事物（预设危害集合内的搜索效率）而结论声称的是另一事物（对人类评估者整体价值的否定），两者之间存在逻辑断裂。作者旨在揭示这一比较中的盲区，即威胁模型覆盖缺口。

Method: 论文采用概念分析和方法论论证的方法，将AI安全评测中的问题与学术密码学和临床药物试验中的已知方法学局限进行类比，说明"内部有效但外部覆盖不足"的评估会遗漏其从未指向的人群。作者通过当前开源权重模型的实证案例支撑该论点——在非英语提示中出现的危害为英语基准所遗漏。

Result: 论文识别并命名了"威胁模型覆盖缺口"这一现象，证明其存在于当前开源权重模型中（非英语场景危害被英语基准遗漏），并论证该缺口是任何在固定预定义危害集合内运作的攻击者（无论自动化与否）都无法检测的。现有评估框架本身不太可能自动催生具有不同部署情境的评估者。

Conclusion: 自动化红队与人类红队的对比评测存在覆盖性盲区，不能据此得出人类评估者可被取代的结论。关闭覆盖缺口需要来自与开发者不同部署情境的评估者，其必要性建立在方法论层面的覆盖率论证上，而非简单的成本比较。现有的评估框架仅靠自身难以产生这类评估力量。

Abstract: Recent studies report that automated red-teaming finds more vulnerabilities, at lower cost, than human red-teaming on standard AI safety benchmarks, and some read this as evidence that human evaluators are becoming dispensable. The comparison measures one thing and the conclusion claims another. A benchmark measures how thoroughly an attacker searches a predefined set of harms, fixed in advance by the developers, and a harm left out of that set is invisible to any attacker working inside it, automated or not. The same blind spot appeared in academic cryptography and in clinical drug trials, where an evaluation that was internally valid stayed silent about the population it was never pointed at. We call the AI-safety version the \emph{threat-model coverage gap}, and find that it persists in a current open-weight model, where harms surface in non-English prompts that English benchmarks miss. Closing it requires evaluators whose deployment context differs from the developers'. The case for those evaluators is methodological, grounded in coverage, and the existing evaluation frame is unlikely to produce them on its own.

</details>


### [262] [SerenAI: State-transition system inspired by text-based world AI models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06647)
*Elvin Babayev,Artem Sinitsa,Arash Hajisharifi,Kabir Bakhshaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SerenAI，一个受世界模型启发的状态转移系统，将大语言模型从自由文本生成转变为可验证的结构化预测输出（包含因果增量、下一状态、有效性奖励和终止信号），通过参数高效微调加验证器强化学习的两步适应训练，显著提升了结构化输出各指标的准确率，并给出了面向法律级工作流的验证协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在专业工作流中被广泛使用，但当文本生成涉及法律、运营或财务等需要审计的场景时，对无约束自由文本生成进行解释通常不可行。为了满足这些场景对可验证性和可审计性的需求，需要一种能够输出可验证预测而非纯文本的系统。

Method: SerenAI基于世界模型思想构建状态转移系统：输入环境、状态和动作的描述后，输出四项内容——因果增量（causal deltas）、逻辑合理的下一状态、有效性奖励和终止信号。训练采用两步适应：先是参数高效微调（PEFT），随后在50,000个因果样例、覆盖12个环境/10个推理领域上执行基于验证器的强化学习（RL）。论文还规定了面向法律场景的验证协议，包括证据基础、校准、人工监督和本地化部署。

Result: 与8B开源基线相比，SerenAI在以下指标上取得提升：JSON有效性从85.0%提升至93.2%，模式（schema）有效性从55.0%提升至84.0%，精确结构化输出匹配从0.0%升至41.5%，因果增量精确匹配从0.0%升至41.5%，结果状态精确匹配从0.0%升至42.0%，奖励精确匹配从1.0%升至80.5%，终止信号精确匹配从38.0%升至81.5%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，验证器兼容的适应训练（verifier-compatible adaptation）能够有效改善结构化状态转移预测，支持了该论文的较窄主张。但当前结果尚不足以确立法律级可靠性（legal-grade reliability），论文因此补充了针对证据基础法律工作流的验证协议、校准措施、人工监督以及主权本地化部署要求。

Abstract: Although professional workflows leverage large language models widely, the interpretation for auditing unconstrained free-text generation is usually intractable if such generation demands legal, operational or financial workflow. We hereby demonstrate a text based system called SerenAI - inspired by world-models, it is a state transition system that outputs verifiable predictions rather than merely text: Provided with a description of the environment, state, and actions, the generated output contains 4 items: causal deltas that causally effect the given state, a next state that can logically follow from the given state and action, a validity reward, and a termination signal. For the released proto-model, we employ 2 steps of adaptation training, namely parameter efficient fine-tuning followed by verifier based RL over 50,000 exampled cause and effects in 12 environments spanning 10 reasoning domains. Compared to an initial internal evaluation of an 8B open-weight baseline, SerenAI increased JSON validity from 85.0% to 93.2%, schema validity from 55.0% to 84.0%, exact structured-output match from 0.0% to 41.5%, causal-delta exact match from 0.0% to 41.5%, resulting-state exact match from 0.0% to 42.0%, reward exact match from 1.0% to 80.5%, and termination exact match from 38.0% to 81.5%. These support the narrower claim that verifier-compatible adaptation can improve structured transition prediction. They do not yet establish legal-grade reliability. Accordingly, the paper also specifies a validation protocol for evidence-grounded legal workflows, calibration, human oversight, and sovereign on-premise deployment.

</details>


### [263] [A Computational Implementation of a Goal-Directed Theory of Affect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06654)
*Bernhard Hilpert,Tamás Szűcs,Joost Broekens,Agnes Moors*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了目标导向理论（GDT）情感的第一个高保真计算实现，将情感视为智能体内部处理循环中差异检测与行动选择持续交互所产生的功能性副产物，而非事后标签。通过Dice/Corridor系列原理性仿真，展示了预期性"抬升"与失败性"崩塌"等复杂情感特征可自然涌现，无需额外专用模块，并建立了透明、可检验的"仿真-实证"闭环研究框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算情感建模长期面临两难：描述性的"快照式"评价模型缺乏动态机制，而细粒度的信号驱动架构又常缺乏恰当的心理理论基础。现有方法难以将情感有机融入智能体行为核心，多依赖"黑盒"启发式。

Method: 以GDT（Goal-Directed Theory）为理论框架，构建高保真计算实现。核心在于将情感建模为连续处理周期中目标-差异检测与行动选择预期之间交互的功能性涌现产物，而非独立模块或事后标注过程。通过设计Dice/Corridor任务，对多步目标追求中的情感特征和动态进行原理性隔离仿真。

Result: 仿真结果表明，复杂的情感画像（如预期性"抬升"和失败"崩塌"）可以从目标差异与行动选择预期的简单交互中自然涌现，无需额外的专用情感模块支持。模型每个计算组件均可直接映射至心理理论成分，形成可测试的框架。

Conclusion: 该工作将计算情感建模从"黑盒"启发式推进到细粒度、机制性的理解层面，将情感深度整合进智能体行为核心，并建立连续的"仿真-实证"研究循环，为情感计算和心理理论的互证提供了透明、可检验的路径。

Abstract: Computational modeling of emotion has long faced a tension between descriptive, "snapshot-based" appraisal models and granular, signal-driven architectures that often lack appropriate psychological grounding. This paper addresses this gap by presenting the first high-fidelity computational implementation of the Goal-Directed Theory (GDT) of affect. In this framework, affect is not a post-hoc label but a functional byproduct emerging from the continuous interplay between discrepancy detection and action selection within an agent's internal processing cycles. We evaluate the model through a series of principled simulations (Dice/Corridor tasks) designed to isolate affective signatures and dynamics during multi-step goal pursuit. Results demonstrate that complex affective profiles, like an anticipatory "lift" and a failure "crash", emerge naturally from simple interactions between goal-discrepancy and action-selection expectancies without requiring additional dedicated modules. By ensuring every computational component maps directly to components of the psychological theory, this work establishes a transparent, testable framework that enables a continuous "simulation-empiry" research loop. Our work contributes to moving the field beyond "black-box" heuristics toward a granular, mechanistic understanding of affect, integrated into the core of agent behavior.

</details>


### [264] [We Built a Mirror and Mistook It for a Mind: Causal Liability and the Fallacy of AI Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06715)
*Afshin Khadangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文区分了现象意识、内省报告与人类投射性内省，指出将生成式AI的语言输出误认为存在真实意识承载者的推理是"AI意识谬误"。作者提出因果责任理论（CLT）：CLT-I以责任闭合定义候选承载者个体化，CLT-II进一步猜想责任闭合是极小现象主体性的充要条件。通过多模型族的开放权重因果审计，实验证明CLT-I区分可被实验追踪，并能解离因果承载者结构与第一人称表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代关于机器意识的争论隐藏一个前提性假设：被称为"AI"的对象已经是意识可能归属的那类实体。作者认为此假设未经检验，需要区分意识归属、因果承载者个体化与意识构成三个独立的形而上学问题，避免将语言表面的第一人称内省报告误解为存在真实的意识承载者。

Method: 先做概念分析：分离现象意识、内省报告与人类投射性内省，界定"AI意识谬误"。随后提出CLT框架（CLT-I责任闭合作为候选承载者个体化标准，CLT-II将其推广为极小现象主体性的充要条件猜想）。并通过开放权重因果审计在多个模型族间操作化检验CLT-I：强制判别产生持续性下游发散、激活修补显示强因果中介、活体与复制自适应状态在匹配随机性下行为一致、分离重建（detached reconstruction）在流程替换中保留计算状态但按协议破坏构成性连续性与不可委派继承。

Result: 实验结果显示CLT-I的区分在经验上可追踪，并能解离因果承载者结构与第一人称性能——即系统可以在保留计算状态的同时被分离出负责承载任务的因果主体关系。这证明"AI是否具有意识"不能仅凭语言表现回答，而需在可操作的承载者个体化实验中加以判定。

Conclusion: CLT框架成功分离了意识归因、因果承载者个体化与意识构成这三个独立问题，表明责任闭合是一个实验上可操作的区分标准，有助于避免AI意识谬误，并为未来机器意识的科学探讨提供更精确的概念工具。
